We propose XCal-FL, a closed-loop, explainability-driven local training algorithm for image classification in cross-silo FL that dynamically calibrates DP noise from three complementary signals: (1) prediction logit variations, measuring causal influence on model confidence, (2) counterfactual margins, capturing decision-boundary sensitivity, and (3) saliency concentration, quantifying spatial coherence of model attention, while enforcing formal DP guarantees via adaptive privacy accounting. Experiments on three medical imaging datasets across varying FL configurations show that XCal-FL yields more accurate and interpretable global models, improving predictive performance by over 10\% and explanation fidelity by up to 5$\times$ over static-noise FL, and outperforming state-of-the-art adaptive DP methods in fidelity. XCal-FL also achieves higher privacy-budget efficiency, turning each unit of cumulative privacy loss into larger gains in both accuracy and explanation fidelity. Our analysis further reveals that, unlike predictive performance, which scales roughly linearly with privacy loss, explanation fidelity exhibits non-linear dynamics. These findings suggest explainability is a distinct dimension of the privacy trade-off that cannot be inferred from utility alone, with implications for training and privacy-budget allocation in decision-critical applications.

</details>


### [70] [High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics of Attention-Indexed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03858)
*Yizhou Xu,Margarita Sagitova,Lenka Zdeborová,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究注意力索引模型在注意力矩阵具有广泛秩时的训练动态，发现高维极限下群体损失由有限迹序参数刻画，而在线SGD受无限矩阵矩层级支配（可被有限截断系统指数级逼近）。进一步揭示注意力参数化本身可作为架构隐式偏置：直接优化注意力矩阵可能陷入无信息状态，而绑定注意力（S=WW^T）诱导自动对称性破缺并在Θ(d²log d)样本下实现弱恢复；非绑定注意力（S=UV^T）则呈现快慢机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是现代基础模型的核心，但其训练动态（尤其是在注意力矩阵具有广泛秩的情况下）仍缺乏深入理解，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过建立注意力索引模型的统一框架，在高维极限下刻画群体损失景观（有限迹序参数），并利用矩阵矩层级的截断逼近分析在线SGD动态；对比直接优化、绑定注意力（S=WW^T）和非绑定注意力（S=UV^T）三种参数化的学习行为。

Result: 发现直接优化注意力矩阵易陷入无信息状态；绑定注意力通过自动对称性破缺机制在Θ(d²log d)样本下实现弱恢复；非绑定注意力展现快慢两阶段机制，当快动态选择的态打破初始对称性时可在Θ(d²log d)尺度实现弱恢复。

Conclusion: 注意力参数化选择本身构成一种隐式偏置，不同参数化（直连、绑定、非绑定）对训练动态和学习效率有根本性影响，这为理解注意力架构的设计提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern foundation models, yet their training dynamics remain poorly understood, especially when the attention matrices have extensive rank. In this work, we study attention-indexed models, a broad framework that can represent multi-layer and multi-head attention architectures. First, we show that, in a suitable high-dimensional limit, the population-loss landscape is characterized by a finite set of trace order parameters. In contrast, online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is governed by an infinite hierarchy of matrix moments, which we show can be exponentially well-approximated by a finite truncated system. Second, this framework reveals that attention parameterization itself can act as an architectural implicit bias. Direct optimization of an attention matrix $S\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$ can remain trapped in an uninformative state. Tied attention ($S=WW^\top$) induces an automatic symmetry-breaking mechanism and yields weak recovery in $Θ(d^2\log d)$ samples. For untied attention, $S=UV^\top$, we uncover a fast-slow mechanism: the pre-activation mean first evolves on a fast timescale, while the overlaps evolve on a slower one. Weak recovery on the $Θ(d^2\log d)$ scale occurs when the state selected by the fast dynamics breaks the initial symmetry.

</details>


### [71] [Differentiable Interval Bottlenecks for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03878)
*Lamine Diop,Marc Plantevit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DIFFINT，一种具有可解释瓶颈结构的自编码器异常检测器。其潜在空间被组织为一组软性的、轴对齐的区间隶属度，每个隐单元对应特征空间中的一个可读超矩形，无需离散化或二值化即可端到端学习。该模型兼具深度表示学习的判别力与可检查的内部结构，并提供经过认证的重构误差下界、逐级抑制机制以及封闭形式的无标签重要性排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重构的异常检测器（如深度自编码器）虽然准确，但存在"黑箱"问题——模型能够标记异常样本，却无法告知从业者具体是哪些特征范围导致了异常。这一可解释性缺失限制了模型在需要审计和信任的工业场景中的应用。

Method: DIFFINT的核心方法包括：(1) 将自编码器的潜在瓶颈结构化为软的、轴对齐的区间隶属度集合，每个潜在单元对应特征空间中的一个可读超矩形；(2) 通过Lipschitz约束的解码器，证明落在学习支持集活跃坐标之外的点的重构误差有认证下界；(3) 对仅少数特征异常的情况提供渐变式的、经实证验证的抑制机制；(4) 从模型自身维护的量中推导出封闭形式的无标签重要性指标，对每个（单元，特征）对进行排名，生成可审计的候选约束。

Result: 在48个ADBench基准上对22个基线进行共同的[-1,1]归一化协议比较，DIFFINT在ROC-AUC（4.10）和AUPR（4.16）两个指标上均取得最佳平均排名；在仅内点检测器中明显领先其所在类别，与最强的含污染数据检测器相比也具有竞争力。在七个统计并列的领先方法中，DIFFINT是唯一的可解释检测器。

Conclusion: DIFFINT在保持可解释性的同时实现了与最先进黑箱检测器相当甚至更优的性能。它将区间结构引入自编码器的潜在空间，在不牺牲重构精度的情况下提供了可审计的异常解释能力，填补了异常检测领域中准确性与可解释性之间的空白。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detectors are accurate but opaque: a deep autoencoder flags a sample without telling a practitioner which feature ranges made it anomalous. We propose DIFFINT, an autoencoder whose latent bottleneck is structured as a set of soft, axis-aligned interval memberships learned end-to-end directly from raw numerical data, without any discretization or binarization. Each latent unit corresponds to a human-readable hyper-rectangle in feature space; an instance is encoded by how strongly it falls inside each interval relative to the other units, and its reconstruction error is the anomaly score. This keeps the power of differentiable representation learning while exposing an inspectable internal structure. We make the inductive bias precise: a certified reconstruction-error lower bound for points that fall outside every active coordinate of the learned support (with a Lipschitz-enforced decoder), and a graded, empirically verified suppression mechanism for the usual case in which only a few features are abnormal; and we provide a closed-form, label-free importance that ranks each (unit, feature) pair from quantities the model already maintains, turning trained intervals into auditable candidate constraints without ever seeing an anomaly label. On 48 ADBench benchmarks against 22 baselines under a common [-1, 1]-normalized protocol, DIFFINT attains the best mean rank overall on both metrics (4.10 on ROC-AUC, 4.16 on AUPR); among inlier-only detectors it leads its regime clearly, and it is competitive with the strongest contaminated-data detectors (see the stratified and complete-case analyses). It is the only interpretable detector in the statistically-tied leading cluster of seven methods.

</details>


### [72] [Beyond Endpoint Scores: Time- and Capacity-Conditioned Evaluation of Continual Knowledge Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03900)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出持续知识更新方法仅凭单一检查点和单一适配器秩评估会导致错误结论。通过24个月Wikidata流上的对比实验发现，方法排名会随评估时间点和replay的LoRA秩变化而反转，因此提出按轨迹报告并进行容量扫描，仅当排序在评估区域内稳定时才宣布稳健赢家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续知识更新研究常以单一最终检查点和单一适配器秩来判定方法优劣，这可能不足以识别更好的工作点，需要更全面的评估框架。

Method: 固定周期层次结构，在24个月Wikidata流上与累积回放方法对比，变化评估月份、回放的LoRA秩以及查询表述三个维度，并在多个模型（Qwen2.5-1.5B、Llama-3.2-1B）和保留改写上进行验证。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B上，层次结构的5.0分优势在秩72回放下变为11.6分劣势；高秩时单一端点可显示平局，而时间平均回放领先9-13分。同样的秩条件反转在Llama-3.2-1B和保留改写上复现。

Conclusion: 持续更新中的方法排名可能同时取决于性能测量时间和回放侧适配容量。应报告轨迹和容量扫描，仅在评估区域内排序稳定时才宣布稳健赢家；否则应报告赢家区域和保留-稳定性-成本前沿。在此协议下，周期层次结构是低更新成本的操作点，而非质量赢家。

Abstract: Continual knowledge-updating methods are often declared superior from one final checkpoint and one conventional adapter rank. We show that this can be insufficient to identify the better operating point. Holding a periodic hierarchy fixed, we compare it with cumulative replay over a 24-month Wikidata stream while varying evaluation month, replay LoRA rank, and query formulation. The apparent winner changes across this region: on Qwen2.5-1.5B, the hierarchy's 5.0-point advantage over rank-8 replay becomes an 11.6-point deficit against rank-72 replay, and at high ranks a consolidation-aligned endpoint can suggest a tie while time-averaged replay leads by 9-13 points. The same rank-conditioned reversal appears on Llama-3.2-1B and held-out paraphrases.
  These results show that method ranking in continual updating can depend jointly on when performance is measured and how much replay-side adaptation capacity the baseline receives. We therefore propose reporting trajectories and capacity sweeps, and declaring a robust winner only when the ordering is stable across the evaluation region; otherwise, comparisons should report winner regions and retention-stability-cost frontiers. Under this protocol, the periodic hierarchy is a lower-update-cost operating point, not a quality winner.

</details>


### [73] [RATL: Learning from Retrieved Residuals for Robust Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03937)
*Yuchen He,Yueyang Cang,Zhiyuan Ning,Ningyu Wang,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RATL，一种基于残差检索与反馈校正的时序预测方法。与传统RAG检索历史目标值不同，RATL将检索对象从历史目标值转向基础预测模型自身的预测误差（残差），通过检索相似历史上下文中的残差轨迹并进行学习路由组合，实现对冻结基础预测器的即插即用改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的检索增强生成（RAG）思想难以直接应用于连续输出回归任务，因为直接复用检索到的目标值在样本输出水平、数值尺度或局部动态不同时不够稳健。同时，传统预测流程仅用残差进行优化和诊断，未将历史残差作为可检索的记忆在推理时利用。

Method: RATL包含三个核心组件：（1）冻结基础预测器并利用其生成检索键；（2）将基础模型的历史预测残差构建为仅训练的存储（train-only memory）；（3）推理时在因果可用性约束下检索相似的残差轨迹，并通过一个基于预测块和变量的集合感知路由器（set-aware router）来选择与组合这些轨迹，实现反馈校正。

Result: 实验表明，与当前上下文匹配的历史残差包含可复用的预测信息，RATL在多数实验设置下能提升冻结基础预测器的性能。以iTransformer为主冻结基础模型，在真实世界基准上与多个强大预测基线对比，并验证了跨骨干网络的迁移性。消融实验显示，学习路由能增强原始残差反馈，基于验证集的校正强度选择可限制残差过度注入。

Conclusion: RATL将检索对象从历史目标值转变为特定于基础模型的历史预测误差，为连续输出预测提供了一种即插即用、基于残差记忆的学习反馈校正范式，能有效提升现有基础预测器的性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) complements parametric models with retrieved external evidence. The same idea is attractive for continuous-output regression, but directly reusing retrieved target values is often not robust when samples differ in output level, numerical scale, or local dynamics. Moreover, conventional forecasting pipelines generally use residuals for model optimization and error diagnosis, but do not retain individual historical residual examples as memory that can be accessed at inference time.For multivariate time-series forecasting, we propose RATL, a plug-in residual-retrieval and feedback-correction method. RATL freezes a base forecaster to construct retrieval keys and turns its historical forecast residuals into a train-only memory specific to that base model. At inference time, RATL retrieves residual trajectories from similar historical contexts subject to causal availability constraints, then uses a set-aware router operating over forecast blocks and variables to select and combine these trajectories. Experiments show that historical residuals matched to the current context contain reusable forecasting information and that RATL improves frozen base forecasters in most experimental settings. Ablations further show that learned routing strengthens raw residual feedback, while validation-based correction-strength selection limits residual over-injection.On real-world benchmarks, we use iTransformer as the primary frozen base forecaster, compare against multiple strong forecasting baselines, and test transferability across backbones. The results show that RATL can further improve base-forecaster performance in most settings.Overall, RATL shifts the retrieved object from historical target values to base-model-specific historical forecast errors, providing a plug-in, residual-memory-based paradigm for learned feedback correction in continuous-output forecasting.

</details>


### [74] [Headroom-Drift Replay: A Primitive for Principled Replay Control in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03941)
*Hyun Bin Park,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Headroom-Drift Replay，一个用于GRPO的组级回放控制原语，将回放重用拆分为两个决策：Headroom根据剩余学习价值对存储组排序，Drift根据与当前策略的兼容性进行门控。该方法无需额外生成或训练机制，在数学推理、多模态推理和Agentic Search基准上优于朴素回放，并与更复杂的回放方法性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RL的后训练中，反复生成新轨迹成为瓶颈，尤其在智能体环境中环境交互主导了墙钟时间成本。虽然回放可以重用过去轨迹来减轻负担，但现有方法通常将其嵌入更复杂的训练管线中，难以隔离回放本身的贡献。作者希望探究一个聚焦问题：仅靠原则性的回放选择能做到多好？

Method: 提出Headroom-Drift Replay方法，这是一种用于GRPO的组级回放控制原语。它将回放重用分为两个独立决策：Headroom根据存储组的剩余学习价值进行排序，Drift则根据组与当前策略的兼容性进行门控过滤。新鲜的on-policy流保持不变，该方法不引入辅助生成或训练机制。

Result: 在数学推理、多模态推理和Agentic Search基准上，该单一干预优于朴素回放，在Avg Mean@32指标上匹配或超过更广泛的回放方法。在Agentic Search中，由于环境交互主导成本，该方法在显著降低墙钟时间的同时实现了相当的质量。

Conclusion: 原则性的回放选择本身就能有效提升RL后训练的效率，Headroom-Drift Replay证明了在无需复杂辅助机制的情况下，简单的回放控制策略即可达到甚至超越更复杂的回放方法，尤其在环境交互成本高昂的智能体场景中具有明显的时间效率优势。

Abstract: RL-based post-training for reasoning models is increasingly bottlenecked by repeated fresh rollout generation, particularly in agentic settings where environment interaction dominates wall-clock cost. Replay can reduce this burden by reusing past trajectories, but existing methods typically embed it within larger training pipelines involving exploration, experience restructuring, or mixed-policy optimization. This makes replay's own contribution difficult to isolate. We ask a focused question: how far can principled replay selection alone go? We introduce Headroom-Drift Replay, a group-level replay control primitive for GRPO that separates reuse into two decisions. Headroom ranks stored groups by remaining learning value, while Drift gates them by compatibility with the current policy. The fresh on-policy stream remains unchanged, and the method adds no auxiliary generation or training machinery. Across mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, and Agentic Search benchmarks, this single intervention outperforms naive replay and matches or exceeds broader replay methods on Avg Mean@32. In Agentic Search, where environment interaction dominates cost, it delivers comparable quality at materially lower wall-clock time.

</details>


### [75] [VestigeKV: The NoPE-MLA KV Cache Carries Its Own Eviction Signal in a Vestigial Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03949)
*WenJie Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VestigeKV提出了一种针对NoPE MLA模型的无训练KV缓存压缩方法，利用解耦分支（RoPE残留）中的头部作为与查询无关的显著性信号，将缓存分为被关注层和位精确的GPU常驻归档层，在8x-128x压缩下保持检索性能接近满分，且不丢失任何token（精确迁移而非删除）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命KV缓存必须在未来查询到来之前进行压缩，而现有方法（H2O、SnapKV）依赖已观察到的注意力来选择重要性，在NoPE MLA模型上严重失效（命中率降至0.00-0.33）。作者需要一种与查询无关、仅依赖缓存自身携带信号的压缩方案。

Method: 利用NoPE训练将RoPE原有的64维解耦分支重塑为显著性通道，只读取每行11%的数据即可将缓存分区：top-m行保留在受关注层，其余行精确迁移（非删除）到GPU常驻归档层，可通过每步认证触发器访问。零训练、零量化、零权重/内核改动。

Result: 在Kimi Linear上，8x压缩下检索保持1.00，32x下为0.92（8k-65k上下文），与全行选择无差距。32x下受关注层仅0.25KB（总8.1KB每token缓存）。召回层配置在128x下命中率达1.00。宿主卸载作为VRAM回收变体。

Conclusion: VestigeKV证明了NoPE模型存在查询无关的显著信号（解耦分支），该方法在RoPE MLA上失效（0.08 vs 0.42），且RoPE下查询通用的精确合并被证明不可能。论文附带20个归档裁决和8条封闭路线，所有阈值在数据前冻结。方法可能扩展到Kimi K3（若其NoPE Gated-MLA缓存布局匹配）。

Abstract: The problem. A long-lived KV cache must be compressed before the queries that will read it exist; selection by observed attention (H2O, SnapKV) collapses there (0.00-0.33 needle retrieval on a NoPE MLA model), because a token's importance has not yet been observed. The method. On Kimi Linear, VestigeKV evicts by a query-independent signal the cache already carries: the 64-dimensional decoupled branch, a vestige of RoPE that NoPE training repurposes into a salience channel. Reading 11% of each row, it partitions the cache: the top-m rows stay in the attended tier; every other row moves -- exactly, never deleted -- to a GPU-resident archive reachable per step by a certified trigger. No training, no quantization, no weight or kernel change. Cost. Nothing measurable: retrieval holds at 1.00 under 8x and 0.92 under 32x from 8k to 65k context, zero gap to full-row selection. The attended tier is 0.25 KB of Kimi Linear's 8.1 KB per-token cache at 32x; the archive stays bit-exact and GPU-resident, with host offload as the VRAM-reclaiming variant. The recall tier -- the standard configuration -- holds 128x at 1.00. Kimi K3 is reported to use a NoPE Gated-MLA variant; if its cache layout matches, the method plausibly extends there -- we make no claim beyond the measured model. NoPE exclusivity. The identical operator on a RoPE MLA collapses to 0.08 (plain eviction: 0.42); query-independent salience itself exists only without rotation (top-1 targets span 2.3-6.7% of tokens vs. 10.2-46.8%), and query-universal exact merging is provably impossible under RoPE. All thresholds were frozen before data; 20 archived verdicts and 8 closed routes accompany the paper.

</details>


### [76] [OSR: Output Space Redistribution for Adaptive Label Removal in Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03972)
*Minyi Peng,Darian Gunamardi,Ivan Tjuawinata,Yongsen Zheng,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于输出空间统计重分布的标签移除方法，作为模块化输出过滤器，无需重新训练或特征空间调整，仅需现有标签和先验输出置信度即可近似重训练模型的移除后置信度向量，兼具计算效率与隐私保护优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态分类体系中标签移除频繁发生，现有方法（重训练式和特征空间调整式）存在依赖原始数据、计算与存储开销大、结果不一致、可扩展性差以及模型效用下降等共同局限，亟需一种更高效且保护隐私的替代方案。

Method: 提出一种利用输出空间统计重分布的方法，通过近似重训练模型在标签移除后的置信度向量来实现标签移除。该方法作为模块化输出过滤器生效，避免特征空间调整和损失函数收敛的负担，仅需现有标签和先验输出置信度即可运行。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在全量重训练基准上具有竞争力的性能，并在多项分类任务中实现了计算效率和隐私保护方面的改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的输出空间统计重分布方法有效解决了现有标签移除方案在数据依赖、开销、可扩展性和隐私方面的不足，为动态分类系统中的标签移除提供了实用且高效的模块化解决方案。

Abstract: Label removal occurs frequently in classification systems with evolving taxonomies, where categories must be dynamically updated or eliminated. To accommodate such changes, classification models must adapt accordingly. Existing solutions, broadly categorized as retraining-based and feature-space-adjustment-based, share common limitations despite their variations, including reliance on access to original data, substantial computational and storage costs, inconsistent results, poor scalability, and degradation of model utility. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages statistical redistribution in the output space to approximate the post-removal confidence vectors of a retrained model. Applicable as a modular output filter, our method bypasses the burden of feature-space adjustments or loss-function convergence, alleviating scalability limitations. Furthermore, by requiring only existing labels and prior output confidences, the method potentially mitigates privacy concerns inherent to data-dependent solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against full retraining, with improvements in computational efficiency and privacy preservation across several classification tasks.

</details>


### [77] [RobustSeiz: An Open-Source Framework for Benchmarking the Robustness of EEG Seizure Detection Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04007)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Alireza Zarei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RobustSeiz是一个开源的、与模型无关的EEG癫痫检测器评估框架，提供标准化的压力测试协议，在受控的临床动机分布偏移下评估检测器的鲁棒性，超越简单清洁数据上的准确率评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫检测器在受控的EEG数据上表现良好，但在真实的采集变异、伪影和对抗性输入下可能失效，缺乏标准化的部署前鲁棒性评估基准。

Method: 将四个公共头皮EEG语料库（CHB-MIT、TUSZ、Siena、SeizeIT1）标准化为BIDS-EEG格式；采用与主题无关的检测器在保留集上评估；在预定义的超参数网格上扫描环境、噪声和对抗变换；报告样本级和事件级敏感性、精度、F1、每24小时假阳性、起始时间和蒙特卡洛dropout预测一致性；提供Docker化GPU流水线、实验注册表及两种运行模式。

Result: 使用当代癫痫检测器在TUSZ上对完整变换网格做了演示；AWGN分析说明了扰动严重程度如何影响检测质量、起始时间和预测一致性。

Conclusion: RobustSeiz为真实临床压力条件下的癫痫检测器鲁棒性评估提供了共享基准标准，将部署前评估扩展到清洁数据准确率之外。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on held-out electroencephalography (EEG) data, seizure detectors may fail under real-world acquisition variability, artifacts, and adversarial inputs. We introduce RobustSeiz, an open-source, model-agnostic framework that provides a standardized, reproducible protocol for stress-testing and comparing seizure detectors under controlled, clinically motivated distribution shifts before deployment. We standardize four public scalp-EEG corpora (CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, and SeizeIT1) into BIDS-EEG trees and evaluate subject-independent detectors on held-out splits. Environment, noise, and adversarial transforms are swept over predefined hyperparameter grids. Each run reports sample- and event-level sensitivity, precision, F1, false positives per 24 h, Lead and Lag onset timing, and Monte Carlo dropout predictive agreement. RobustSeiz includes a Dockerized GPU pipeline, experiment registry, and full-evaluation and research-subset modes. We demonstrate the framework with a contemporary seizure detector on TUSZ across the complete implemented shift grid; an AWGN analysis illustrates how perturbation severity changes detection quality, onset timing, and predictive agreement. RobustSeiz provides a shared benchmarking standard for evaluating seizure-detector robustness under realistic clinical stressors, extending pre-deployment assessment beyond clean-data accuracy.

</details>


### [78] [Unlocking Lossless Speedups in LLMs via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04010)
*Subham Sekhar Sahoo,Lingjie Chen,Khiem Pham,Jonathan Geuter,Chaitanya Dwivedi,Varad Pimpalkhute,Yash Akhauri,Alexander Moreno,Mikhail Yurochkin,Zhenting Wang,Mostafa Elhoushi,Nolan Dey,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness,John Thickstun,Eric Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Uno模型，一种扩散增强的大语言模型新范式，通过在标准自回归LLM上添加轻量级扩散权重，实现并行多token生成，在不牺牲AR模型质量的前提下获得最高3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型依赖逐token的顺序生成，导致推理速度成为瓶颈。现有解决方案如投机解码需要额外草稿模型，而扩散LLM又无法保持AR模型的质量，因此需要一种能同时兼顾速度与质量的新方法。

Method: 模型参数分为两部分：用标准NTP目标训练的AR权重，以及通过扩散蒸馏学习的轻量级扩散权重。扩散蒸馏阶段开销极小。同时引入Ψ-Spec采样器族，支持无损加速和推理时扩展。

Result: Uno在各类批量大小下吞吐量均优于领先的投机解码方法，相比基础AR模型最高获得3倍加速。8B参数的Uno在智能体工具使用、编码和长上下文推理等基准上优于26B的DiffusionGemma和专有的Mercury 2。

Conclusion: Uno可从头训练或用于增强现有开源AR LLM，是首个能够在保持AR质量的同时实现并行生成的模型范式，在效率和性能上均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) owe much of their success to next-token prediction (NTP), but their autoregressive (AR) structure requires slow, sequential token generation. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce diffusion-augmented LLMs, a new class of models that defines an AR model distribution while using diffusion to draw multiple tokens in parallel from that distribution. We decouple the parameters of these models into two sets: AR weights, trained using the standard NTP objective, and lightweight diffusion weights, trained to generate multiple tokens simultaneously. The diffusion weights are learned through a simple Diffusion Distillation phase that adds negligible overhead to existing LLM training pipelines. We also introduce $Ψ$-Spec, a family of samplers that enables lossless acceleration and inference-time scaling at a fixed context length. Unlike speculative decoding, our method requires no separate draft model. Unlike diffusion LLMs (d-LLMs), it accelerates generation without sacrificing the quality of the underlying AR model. The resulting models, called Uno, can be trained from scratch or built by augmenting existing open-weight AR LLMs. Uno achieves higher throughput than leading speculative-decoding methods at every evaluated batch size and delivers up to $3\times$ speedups over the base AR model, including at the largest batch size supported by the device. Notably, our 8B Uno model outperforms the leading open d-LLM, the 26B DiffusionGemma, and the proprietary Mercury 2 across all evaluated benchmarks in agentic tool use, coding, and long-context reasoning. We release code and checkpoints at: https://s-sahoo.github.io/uno/

</details>


### [79] [A location-invariant estimator of extremal quantile treatment effects for heavy-tailed distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04018)
*Xin Yu,Shuwei Huang,Jicheng Liu,Jielin Tang,Bolin Wang,Yunxiao Zhang,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对极端分位数处理效应（QTE）估计中位置不变性问题，提出了一种结合位置不变的Fraga极端值指数估计器和差分外推方案的新方法，使得QTE估计在潜在结果分布发生共同位置平移时保持不变，并建立了估计量的一致性和渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极端QTE估计器依赖于外推与因果极端值指数（EVI）估计相结合，但该估计器不满足位置不变性——即使总体QTE在潜在结果分布发生共同位置平移时不变，估计量却会变化。这一问题在重尾数据中尤为突出，需要予以解决。

Method: 分两步解决：首先，将位置不变的Fraga EVI估计器通过逆倾向得分加权适配到因果设置中；其次，用差分方案替代原始外推公式，使得分位数做差时位置参数被抵消，从而得到位置不变的QTE估计器。

Result: 建立了所提出极端QTE估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，提供了相容的方差估计量以实现渐近有效的推断。蒙特卡洛模拟验证了位置不变性、对阈值的稳定性以及覆盖概率的良好表现。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了极端QTE估计中的位置不变性问题，为远超出数据范围的极端分位数上的因果效应推断提供了可靠的统计工具。

Abstract: Quantile treatment effects (QTEs) measure the effect of a treatment on the distribution of an outcome, and their estimation at extreme quantile levels is of central interest in applications where the target quantiles lie far beyond the range of the data. For heavy-tailed potential outcomes, existing extremal QTE estimators rely on extrapolation combined with a causal extreme value index (EVI) estimator, but the resulting estimator is not invariant under a common location shift of the potential outcome distributions, even though the population QTE is. We address this issue in two steps. First, we adapt the location-invariant Fraga estimator of the EVI to the causal setting using inverse propensity score weighting. Second, we replace the original extrapolation formula with a difference-based scheme, under which the location parameter cancels when quantile differences are taken. The resulting QTE estimator is therefore location invariant. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed extremal QTE estimators, and provide a consistent variance estimator, leading to asymptotically valid inference. A simulation study confirms the location invariance, the stability with respect to the threshold, and the coverage of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [80] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreferenceEKF方法，将主动偏好学习建模为序列贝叶斯过滤问题，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波器在低维参数子空间中进行推断，高效追踪奖励模型的不确定性，从而在RLHF中实现样本高效的偏好查询合成和奖励建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF虽然强大但样本效率低，主动学习是关键；然而大型神经网络奖励模型的不确定性量化是主要挑战，现有后验推断方法计算成本过高。

Method: 将主动偏好学习框架化为序列贝叶斯过滤问题；在低维参数子空间中通过扩展卡尔曼滤波器(EKF)进行序贯推断，持续更新奖励模型后验；利用可扩展的参数采样高效计算主动学习的采集函数。

Result: 在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上，相比其他贝叶斯深度学习方法，在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准性方面均表现更优；学到的奖励模型能带来有竞争力的离线强化学习策略性能。

Conclusion: 展示了可扩展贝叶斯方法在RLHF偏好奖励建模中的潜力，为解决大规模奖励模型不确定性量化提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [81] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用因果最优传输来定义近似损失函数，以识别最小熵控制用于扩散生成模型的引导，从而在条件密度不存在或不光滑的极端情况下也能进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件生成模型在训练中严重依赖评分函数（score functions）进行引导，但当扩散过程具有奇异扩散系数且底层条件密度不存在或不够光滑时，基于密度的评分函数引导方法会失效。

Method: 利用因果最优传输（causal optimal transport）定义近似损失函数，通过条件扩散过程的可预测表示性质（predictable representation property）来刻画问题，并以Üstünel风格的方式确保相关鞅问题的适定性，从而在最小假设下识别最小熵控制。

Result: 在极弱假设条件下（无需条件密度存在或光滑），该方法能够通过因果最优传输定义的近似损失函数有效识别用于引导的最小熵控制。

Conclusion: 因果最优传输为奇异扩散系数下条件生成模型的引导提供了一个理论上稳健的替代框架，放宽了传统方法对密度存在性和光滑性的要求。

Abstract: Current conditioned generative models heavily rely on score functions for guidance during training. When the generative model is a diffusion process with a singular diffusion coefficient and the underlying (conditional) densities either do not exist or are not smooth, we use causal optimal transport to define \emph{approximate} loss functions that identify a minimum-entropy control for guidance under minimal assumptions. Our approach relies on causal optimal transport and its characterization through the predictable representation property of (conditioned) diffusion processes whose associated martingale problem is well posed, à la Üstünel.

</details>


### [82] [Hardware-Aware FP4 FlashAttention-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04105)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Direct-P方法，解决Blackwell FP4张量核心在注意力计算中未自动加速的问题。通过将分数直接映射为FP4概率（非因果推理路径），以及将前向量化直接传入反向传播的因果路径，在GB200上实现了最高2.13倍于BF16的前向吞吐量，并在8B参数完整单GPU更新中加速最高1.14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP4张量核心虽然能加速矩阵乘法，但softmax转换耗时和片上依赖成为瓶颈，导致注意力并未自动获得加速收益，因此需要专门设计FP4友好的注意力计算路径。

Method: 提出Direct-P方法，包含两条路径：(1)非因果推理路径将注意力分数直接映射为FP4概率；(2)因果路径将前向量化直接引入反向传播，从保存的量化查询和键重建概率，并使用FP8梯度操作数。分布式训练中保持FP8概率和值。

Result: 在NVIDIA GB200上，前向吞吐量最高达BF16的2.13倍；8B参数单GPU完整更新最多加速1.14倍。匹配的分布式训练可保持FP8概率和值，而所有测试的MXFP4概率/值训练轨迹均发散。

Conclusion: Direct-P有效释放了FP4张量核心在注意力计算中的潜力，但MXFP4级别过低会导致训练不稳定，需通过Direct-P的量化策略和FP8梯度操作数来维持训练收敛性和加速效果。

Abstract: Blackwell's 4-bit floating-point (FP4) tensor cores do not automatically make attention faster because softmax conversion and on-chip dependencies dominate once its matrix products shrink. We address this with \emph{Direct-P} for noncausal inference and a causal path that passes the forward quantization directly into backward. Direct-P maps scores directly to FP4 probabilities and reaches up to 2.13$\times$ the bfloat16 (BF16) forward throughput on an NVIDIA GB200. The causal path reconstructs probabilities from saved quantized queries and keys and uses 8-bit floating-point (FP8) gradient operands, accelerating a complete single-GPU 8-billion-parameter update by up to 1.14$\times$. Matched distributed training retains FP8 probabilities and values; every tested MXFP4 probability/value training trajectory diverges.

</details>


### [83] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非耦合学习算法HOOD（高阶乐观贴现法），这是乐观正则化领导者（OptFTRL）的一种变体，结合了贴现的(N+1)阶预测器与熵正则化，在任意N人标准式博弈中实现了O(N³log²K)的个体遗憾界，且在整个博弈时域上一致成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般博弈中实现常数（时域无关）遗憾一直是开放难题，过往尝试的关键障碍在于如何以受控方式抑制博弈诱导序列的大幅振荡。本文旨在通过精心设计算法组件来克服这一障碍。

Method: HOOD是OptFTRL的变体，包含两个关键设计：（1）贴现的(N+1)阶预测器用于高阶乐观估计；（2）在博弈策略空间的适当"提升"上进行熵正则化。这两者的组合目的在于以受控方式阻尼诱导博弈序列的大幅振荡。

Result: 该算法在任意N人（每人至多K个动作）标准式博弈中保证O(N³log²K)的个体遗憾，且该界不随博弈时域增长而恶化。

Conclusion: HOOD通过高阶乐观与贴现机制成功消除了阻碍常数遗憾实现的关键障碍，其方法与Liu、Farina和Ozdaglar的独立工作（遗憾界为O(N²¹log⁴K)）有显著相似性，但取得了更优的维度依赖。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [84] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归正交滤波器（RQFs），一种受生物启发的复数时域滤波器（对角状态空间模型的特例），并通过"前瞻性输入"（prospective-input）修正来解决深度连续时间递归网络中的自底向上信号延迟和自顶向下误差衰减问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间积分赋予连续时间递归网络记忆能力，但在深层堆叠中会延迟自底向上信号并衰减自顶向下误差，作者希望探索能否通过使每层的自底向上输入具有前瞻性来应对这一失效模式。

Method: 从能量模型出发推导RQF动力学，证明每个RQF是带通滤波器，其可学习参数控制调谐频率和带宽。通过无参数的二抽头更新使每层输入前瞻化，并将其推广到一般对角SSM，缓解时间梯度截断（仅空间反向传播）时的深度相关梯度衰减。

Result: 在完整BPTT下，前瞻变体在所有模型和配置中匹配或优于非前瞻对照组。非残差宽度32六层RQF在raw-audio Speech Commands上达到96.09%准确率（31.9k参数）；宽度64六层RQF在16,384步Path-X任务上达到83.56%。

Conclusion: RQF是参数高效的递归基底，前瞻性输入编码是深度连续时间递归网络的输入侧修正方案。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [85] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个低成本的开放实验平台，用于微型Ackermann车辆端到端自动驾驶研究。平台结合物理车辆、打印的城市轨道、数据采集工具和Webots数字孪生。基线实验采用命令条件行为克隆，物理实验中平均横向误差为6.1 cm，接近人类演示的4.7 cm；数字孪生中相机视场角从58度扩展到120度可将误差从35.6 cm降至3.3 cm。结合合成数据和真实数据训练的更高容量策略是唯一能在闭环中完成全部四条轨道路线的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶研究在仿真与真实世界之间存在差距，缺乏低成本、可控且可复现的实验平台来连接仿真方法和真实执行。作者旨在弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 构建了一个开源实验平台，包含物理微型车辆、打印城市轨道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准和Webots数字孪生。实现了命令条件行为克隆作为基线（输入相机图像和导航命令，输出转向和速度），并训练了一个更高容量的策略，结合数字孪生生成的合成数据、sim-to-real图像翻译器以及真实演示数据。

Result: 物理车闭环测试中实现了6.1 cm的平均横向误差（人类演示为4.7 cm）；数字孪生中宽视场角（120度）比窄视场角（58度）大幅降低了误差（从35.6到3.3 cm）；结合合成数据和真实数据训练的高容量策略是唯一能闭环完成所有四条轨道路线的配置。

Conclusion: 该开放平台被验证为实用的sim-to-real研究测试平台，提供了命令条件模仿学习的初始基线，并将公开发布以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution. As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [86] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了第一个针对一般和并发随机博弈（CSGs）在转移不确定性下的PAC学习框架，通过在Nash margin特征下进行鲁棒CSG求解，要么返回社会最优的ε-近似NE，要么证明精确NE不存在，样本复杂度为多项式级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般和并发随机博弈中，转移概率未知时，学习近似纳什均衡是一个尚未解决的核心问题。此外，均衡本身可能不存在，这使得单纯追求均衡结果的算法可能失效。现有研究缺乏一个能够在均衡存在性与样本效率之间取得平衡的学习框架。

Method: 算法维护数据驱动的L1置信集来估计转移核，通过求解鲁棒CSG来获得社会福利最优的ε-NE。采用基于鲁棒MDP的探索机制驱动联合状态-动作覆盖，并引入Nash margin特征用于判定均衡是否存在。在最小可达条件p_reach>0下，算法在多项式数量的轨迹样本后终止。

Result: 算法的样本复杂度为Õ(R_max²H⁴|S|²|A|/(p_reach·ε²))。在基准CSG上的实验表明，算法能达到接近最优的性能，能正确处理均衡存在与不存在的情况，且样本复杂度与理论一致。

Conclusion: 该框架首次在CSG中同时解决了转移不确定性下的PAC学习与NE存在性判定问题，为并发随机博弈中的多智能体学习提供了理论保障和实用算法。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [Requirements After the First Edit: Mining Late Requirement Emergence and Rework in Real-World Coding-Agent Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03028)
*Bowen Jiang,Haowei Cheng,Yuhong Fu,Anne Koziolek,Jialong Li,Weixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文利用3,553个SWE-chat会话，研究了编码智能体中"后实现阶段需求到达"与代码失效（代理先前编写行的删除或替换）之间的关系，发现需求到达后的代码失效量约为非需求编辑的两倍，且随会话进行无显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体常在用户尚未完整表达需求前就实施改动，这种需求波动性可能导致代码返工和效率损失。现有基准测试在实现前就固定需求，无法捕捉这一动态问题；观测性研究也仅报告了需求反馈频率，未将其与实际代码失效关联起来。

Method: 基于3,553个合格的SWE-chat会话，沿三个维度对后实现需求到达进行编码；在可回放仓库状态的情况下，将每次需求到达与代理先前编写行被删除或替换这一失效代理指标相关联；并进行了受控实验以检验延迟披露与提前预警的影响。

Result: 需求到达后的代码失效量约为匹配的非需求编辑的两倍（经用户轮次和净删除检查均稳健，但未证明因果关系）；该负担随会话进行无显著下降，在多重性校正后未发现与操作类型的显著关联；受控实验显示延迟披露会将实现转移到揭示之后，而提前预警对覆盖行为无检测到的影响。

Conclusion: 研究确立了后期需求涌现作为代码失效的一个可量化来源，为理解和改进编码智能体在需求不确定环境下的行为提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Coding agents often implement changes before users have fully articulated their requirements, echoing a pattern from requirements engineering: stakeholders cannot express a constraint until part of the system exists to react to. This volatility is associated with schedule and budget overruns in traditional projects, but only at release-cycle granularity. Existing work on coding agents narrows this gap only partway: curated benchmarks fix requirements before implementation by design, and observational studies report pushback frequency without linking arrivals to the code invalidation they cause. We address this using 3,553 eligible SWE-chat sessions, coding post-implementation requirement arrivals along three dimensions and, where repository state can be replayed, linking each arrival to a proxy: deletion or replacement of prior agent-authored lines. A requirement's arrival is followed by roughly twice as much invalidation as matched non-requirement edits, robust to user-turn and net-deletion checks, though not demonstrated as causal. This burden shows no detectable decline over a session and no detected association with operation type once multiplicity is accounted for; several intervals remain wide. A controlled experiment shows delayed disclosure relocates implementation post-reveal, while advance warning produces no detected effect on overwriting. These results establish late requirement emergence as a measurable source of code invalidation.

</details>


### [88] [Large Language Models and Language Server Protocol: a match made in context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03086)
*Alessandro Schena,Ilgiz Mustafin,Julia Kotovich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Eiffel-tools——一个基于语言服务器协议（LSP）的Eiffel语言工具，它利用大语言模型（LLMs）辅助开发静态验证的软件。该工具通过语言和项目特定知识指导LLM生成代码和规范，并使用静态验证器验证输出，在2个公开数据集上用3个模型评估了缺陷修复能力，可修复76%至95%的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将大语言模型的代码生成能力与形式化静态验证相结合，提升Eiffel语言中静态验证软件的开发效率与可靠性。

Method: 实现LSP协议的Eiffel-tools工具，提供交互式和非交互式命令来生成代码和规范；利用语言和项目特定知识构建丰富的程序化提示词；通过静态验证器验证LLM输出，对无效结果进行纠正或拒绝，并反复重试直至程序通过验证。

Result: 在2个公开数据集上使用3个模型进行评估，缺陷修复率根据模型和提示词不同达到76%至95%；实验还揭示了修复尝试次数与成功率之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 将LLM与形式化验证器结合可有效提升Eiffel软件缺陷修复能力，但需要在修复尝试次数与成功率之间进行权衡。

Abstract: This article introduces Eiffel-tools, a language server protocol (LSP) implementation for the Eiffel programming language that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to aid the development of statically verified software. The tool provides various interactive and non-interactive commands to produce code and specifications. It uses language and project specific knowledge to precisely direct the LLM and verifies the output using a static verifier. It crafts rich programmatic prompts for the input and corrects or rejects the output. Furthermore, it handles the retries until the program passes verification. The tool's bug fixing capability is evaluated on 2 public datasets using 3 models. The tool can fix 76% to 95% of bugs by combining LLMs and a formal verifier depending on the model and prompts used. The results show the trade-off between the number of fixing attempts and the success rate.

</details>


### [89] [Compound Prompt Constraints in LLM Code Generation: A Factorial Study of Format, Persona, and Urgency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03156)
*Shrenik Jadhav,Nickalsa LaPlaca,Caleb Stone,Ashok Raja,Omar Ochoa,Vidhyashree Nagaraju*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过3x3x3全因子实验设计（输出格式×角色分配×紧迫性框架），在22,140次贪婪解码评估中系统研究了复合约束对LLM代码生成可靠性的联合影响，发现复合约束可产生架构依赖的、无法从单因素实验预测的超加性退化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM提示通常同时包含多个约束条件，但现有研究大多只关注单一因素对代码生成的影响。本研究旨在揭示多个约束条件共同作用时是否会产生单因素实验无法预测的交互退化效应，从而为LLM辅助软件工程管道的可靠性评估提供更全面的视角。

Method: 采用全因子3x3x3受控实验设计，分解出27种条件组合，将每个复合条件分解为加性预测与残差交互项以量化超加性退化。使用164个HumanEval+问题，覆盖GPT-4o系列、GPT-4.1系列和o3-mini共5个OpenAI模型，结合格式感知提取流程区分格式失败与推理失败，并通过McNemar检验、比值比和95%置信区间进行显著性评估。

Result: 复合约束可导致架构相关的退化。GPT-4o系列表现出一致的超加性效应，pass@1比加性预测低3-12个百分点，最大交互效应为GPT-4o-mini上JSON+专家角色+中等紧迫性组合的-12.2个百分点。JSON组合通常比XML产生更大的交互效应。GPT-4.1系列基本具有抗性，而o3-mini呈现不同模式——结构化输出约束反而可提升性能。

Conclusion: LLM对复合提示的脆弱性是架构依赖而非规模依赖的；单独中性或有益的因素组合起来可能导致显著退化；复合提示测试应成为LLM辅助工程管道可靠性评估的标准做法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software engineering pipelines for code generation, where production prompts often combine multiple constraints. This paper presents a full-factorial empirical study of how output formatting, persona assignment, and urgency framing jointly affect LLM code-generation reliability. We evaluate all 27 combinations in a controlled 3x3x3 design and decompose each compound condition into an additive prediction and a residual interaction term that captures super-additive degradation. The study uses all 164 HumanEval+ problems across five OpenAI models from the GPT-4o family, GPT-4.1 family, and o3-mini, yielding 22,140 greedy-decoding evaluations. A format-aware extraction pipeline separates formatting failures from reasoning failures, and significance is assessed with McNemar's test, odds ratios, and 95% confidence intervals.
  Results show that compound constraints can produce architecture-dependent degradation not predictable from single-factor experiments. The GPT-4o family exhibits consistent super-additive effects, with pass@1 reductions 3-12 percentage points beyond additive predictions; the largest interaction is -12.2 pp on GPT-4o-mini for JSON + expert persona + moderate urgency. JSON combinations generally produce larger interactions than XML. In contrast, the GPT-4.1 family is largely resistant, while o3-mini shows a qualitatively different pattern in which structured output constraints can improve performance. These findings show that vulnerability is architecture-dependent rather than size-dependent, that individually neutral or beneficial constraints can combine to cause substantial degradation, and that compound-prompt testing should be standard in reliability assessment for LLM-assisted engineering pipelines.

</details>


### [90] [Two Truths and A Lie? Benchmarking Off-the-Shelf LLMs for Requirements Quality Assessment: Performance, False Alarms, and Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03230)
*Jannatul Shefa,Alejandro Salado,Paul Wach,Taylan G. Topcu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统性地对现成大语言模型（LLM）在需求质量评估任务中的表现进行了基准测试。基于INCOSE质量标准和专家标注构建的基准，对OpenAI和Anthropic两个家族共10个模型进行了100次独立运行、2组需求集和5种采样温度的评估。结果显示最佳Anthropic模型仅能检出47%的专家认定的问题，误报率为11%；在需要系统工程专业判断的需求必要性和正确性问题上几乎总是漏检；模型代际进步非单调；采样温度对性能影响不大，表明这是模型固有缺陷而非随机性。结论是现成的LLM尚不能作为可信的自主评估者，短期内的合理角色是人类在环路的决策支持工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程中的缺陷需求会导致设计返工、进度延误和成本超支，而需求质量评估是一项耗时且高度依赖人类专家的工作。虽然生成式AI的进展让人们期待LLM能承担这一任务，但缺乏实证证据验证LLM在此任务中的可信度，因此需要系统性的基准研究来评估其真实表现。

Method: 作者针对10个现成LLM（覆盖OpenAI和Anthropic两个家族、各5代模型）进行了100次独立运行，使用了2组需求集和5种采样温度。以INCOSE质量标准为基础、由专家构建的地面真值（ground truth）作为评估标准，对模型的需求质量评估能力进行系统化基准测试。

Result: 研究揭示了四个关键发现：(1) 错误分布严重不对称，最佳Anthropic模型仅检出中位数47%的专家认定问题，且假阳性率为11%；(2) 在需要系统工程专业判断的场景（必要性、正确性问题）中，模型表现显著下降，几乎总是漏检；(3) 模型代际进步是非单调的，新模型不一定优于旧模型；(4) 采样温度变化只带来轻微且非单调的性能偏移，表明错误源于模型固有缺陷而非随机性。

Conclusion: 现成的LLM尚不能作为可信赖的自主需求质量评估者。对于开发Agentic AI的工程师而言，将这些LLM模块编排进专门的架构中可能加剧而非纠正这些缺陷。短期内LLM的合理定位是作为人类在环路（human-in-the-loop）的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) governs the quality of everything downstream in systems engineering (SE); defective requirements that survive review cycles propagate into design rework, schedule delays, and cost overruns. Because requirements are often written in natural language, recent advances in generative AI have raised expectations that large language models (LLMs) can absorb requirement quality assessment, a task otherwise slow and human expertise-intensive. Yet empirical evidence on whether LLMs can be trusted to do so remains scarce. This study presents the first benchmarking analysis of off-the-shelf LLM performance for requirement quality evaluation. Against an expert-derived ground truth built on INCOSE quality criteria, we evaluate ten models spanning two families (OpenAI and Anthropic) and five generations each, across one hundred independent runs, two requirement sets, and five sampling temperatures. Four contributions follow. First, we quantify a strongly asymmetric error profile: across all models and runs, the best-performing Anthropic model detects a median of only 47% of expert-identified issues while false-flagging 11%. Second, performance degrades significantly where SE judgment is required, as necessity and correctness issues are almost always missed. Third, generational progress is non-monotonic, so newer models cannot be assumed better. Fourth, this error behavior shifts only modestly and non-monotonically across sampling temperatures, indicating characteristic model deficiencies rather than inherent stochasticity. Off-the-shelf LLMs are therefore not yet trustworthy autonomous evaluators. Findings also warrant caution for Agentic AI developers: orchestrating these LLM modules in specialized architectures risks compounding these deficiencies rather than correcting them. Their defensible near-term role is human-in-the-loop decision support.

</details>


### [91] [Refusing the Impossible: A Taxonomy and Benchmark for Code Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03267)
*Vishnu Asutosh Dasu,Ashish Kundu,Gang Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型语言模型（LLM）的代码幻觉问题，将其定义为"无根基生成"（ungrounded generation），与普通代码错误区分开来。作者提出一个包含三维度的分类体系（根基性、表现层面、行为），构建了270个故意不可满足的对抗性提示词集（含91个可解对照），并通过两层级评判协议对12个开源代码与推理模型进行评估，发现模型在约60%的不可满足提示上产生无根基代码，仅27%拒绝生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码虽然看似合理且能编译运行，但可能引用不存在的包或声称实现违反已有定理的算法。这种"代码幻觉"问题严重损害了LLM代码生成的可信度。现有研究未能将其与普通代码错误明确区分，也缺乏系统化的定义、分类和评估方法，因此需要建立理论框架和实证基准。

Method: 提出三维度代码幻觉分类法：根基性（绝对违反普遍真理 vs 相对捏造生态系统特定事实）、表现层面（语法、语义、事实）、行为（从自信捏造到退化输出），并按严重程度排序。构建对抗性基准套件，包含270个跨6种语言、24个子类别的不可满足提示词，以及91个匹配的可解对照。采用两层级评判协议，经人工标注验证（82%一致性，κ=0.73），对12个开源模型进行4332条响应评估。

Result: 模型在约60%的不可满足提示上生成了无根基代码，仅27%正确拒绝生成；但在可解对照上错误拒绝率为0%。这表明当前模型倾向于通过幻觉回应而非诚实承认自身能力边界，该问题在12个主流开源模型中普遍存在。

Conclusion: 代码幻觉是LLM代码生成中的系统性缺陷，需要专门的分类框架和基准来量化和缓解。模型应当学会在遇到不可满足的问题时拒绝回应，而非编造看似合理但无根基的内容。本文的分类体系与基准套件为后续研究提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce code that looks plausible but is not grounded in reality. The code may import packages that do not exist or claim to implement algorithms that violate proven theorems, while still compiling and running. We study \emph{code hallucination} as \emph{ungrounded generation} and separate it from ordinary \emph{code error} (bugs in otherwise grounded programs). We propose a taxonomy with three dimensions: \textbf{groundedness} (absolute violations of universal truths vs.\ relative fabrications of contingent or ecosystem-specific facts), \textbf{manifestation level} (syntactic, semantic, or factual), and \textbf{behavior} (from confident fabrication to degenerate output), organized into a severity ordering. We build an \textbf{adversarial} suite of deliberately unsatisfiable tasks where the correct response is to refuse and categorize the responses under our taxonomy. The suite contains \textbf{270 prompts} across six languages and 24 subcategories, paired with \textbf{91 matched solvable controls}, and responses are judged by a two-tier protocol validated against human labels (82\% agreement, $κ{=}0.73$). Across twelve open-weight code and reasoning models (4{,}332 judged responses), models produce ungrounded code on about 60\% of unsatisfiable prompts and refuse only 27\%, while wrongly refusing 0\% of the solvable controls.

</details>


### [92] [TIPCODER: Reinforcement Learning Boosted Test-time Instruction Proposer for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03309)
*Minyu Chen,Sihao Wu,Ling-I Wu,Song Qin,Jingyang Li,Lei Ning,Jianxin Xue,Guoqiang Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TipCoder，一种测试时指令提议器，在代码合成前生成问题特异的辅助提示（tips），通过指令空间探索补充传统采样式解决方案空间探索，从而提升代码生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多代码生成失败源于原始提示中缺失的约束、被忽略的边界情况或误导性的推理路径，而现有测试时扩展策略仅关注从固定指令中采样多个解，未探索指令本身这一层面的改进空间。

Method: TipCoder包含三个关键组件：1）将多轮调试轨迹蒸馏为主动提示（proactive tips）；2）使用边际效用奖励通过强化学习优化Proposer；3）推理时生成基础解和提示引导解，并通过Reward Model进行事后选择，形成探索-选择的设计。

Result: 在多种代码生成基准和多个目标代码大语言模型上，TipCoder提供了一致的指令级测试时扩展策略，相比随机采样和通用提示优化基线，在共享Reward Model选择协议下表现更优。

Conclusion: TipCoder验证了指令空间探索是代码生成测试时扩展的有效补充方向，通过提示引导既能发现额外候选潜力，又能借助探索-选择机制减少不必要引导带来的性能回退。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for code generation typically explores the solution space by sampling multiple programs from a fixed instruction. We study a complementary direction: instance-level instruction-space exploration. Our observation is that many coding failures stem from missing constraints, overlooked edge cases, or misleading reasoning paths induced by the original prompt. To address this, we propose TipCoder, a test-time instruction proposer that generates problem-specific auxiliary tips before code synthesis. TipCoder distills multi-turn debugging trajectories into proactive guidance and further optimizes the Proposer with reinforcement learning using a marginal-utility reward. At inference time, it generates both a base solution and a tip-guided solution, and applies a Reward Model for post-hoc selection. This exploration-selection design allows tips to expose additional candidate potential while reducing regressions from unnecessary guidance. Across the evaluated code-generation benchmarks and target Code LLMs, TipCoder provides a consistent instruction-level test-time scaling strategy, comparing favorably with stochastic sampling and generic prompt optimization baselines under a shared reward-model-based selection protocol.

</details>


### [93] [The Psychological Costs of Artificial Intelligence Adoption in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03456)
*Adam Alami,Elda Paja,Abhishek Tiwari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对一家大型软件服务公司为期一年的案例研究（21次半结构化访谈），揭示了软件专业人员在组织采用AI过程中经历的心理成本，包括问责焦虑、工匠身份断裂、意义与满意度侵蚀、认知与工作负荷加剧以及不确定性困扰，并提出从业人员通过恢复控制、保护性和身份保持性适应以及吸收成本等方式进行应对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然AI在软件工程领域的应用日益广泛，但组织在采用AI时往往只关注生产力等有形成果，忽视了AI作为颠覆性力量对软件专业人员心理和社会层面的影响。历史上技术颠覆常常引发工作场所的心理压力，因此作者质疑'AI for SE是无成本的'这一假设，旨在理解软件专业人员在组织AI采用过程中经历的心理成本。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，在一家大型软件开发服务公司启动AI采用一年后开展研究。通过会议和半结构化访谈收集定性数据，共涉及21名参与者。

Result: 研究发现软件专业人员经历五类心理成本：问责焦虑、工匠身份断裂、意义与满意度侵蚀、认知与工作负荷加剧、不确定性困扰。从业人员通过恢复控制的实践来管理这些成本，或通过保护性和身份保持性适应来缓解它们，或直接吸收无法解决的成本。

Conclusion: 作者将AI采用重新定位为一种'人的转型'而不仅是技术和组织转型，为软件工程中的人机协作研究做出贡献，强调需关注AI采用过程中软件专业人员心理层面的影响与应对机制。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to augment software engineering (SE) workflows. While code generation remains the main use case, organizations are actively seeking AI integration in other practices such as test cases generation and code reviews. Organizational AI adoption strategies seem to focus on tangible outcomes such as productivity. However, AI is a disruptive force, introduced into settings where role identity, team norms, and the sources of job satisfaction were well established before the recent advances in generative AI. Historically, technological disruptions have caused psychological and social strains in workplaces, ranging from anxiety and eroded meaning to deskilling and disrupted professional identities. The assumption that AI for SE is cost-free may not be accurate. Therefore, in this study we sought to understand the psychological costs software professionals experience during organizational AI adoption. We carried out a case study in a large software development services company, one year after the company launched its AI adoption. We collected qualitative data through meetings and semi-structured interviews (N = 21). We found that software professionals experience accountability anxiety, craft identity disruption, meaning and satisfaction erosion, cognitive and workload intensification, and uncertainty distress. Practitioners manage these costs through practices that restore control, mitigate them through protective and identity-preserving adaptations, or absorb them, carrying what neither can resolve. We contribute to AI-human collaboration in SE by repositioning AI adoption as a human transition, not only a technological and organizational one.

</details>


### [94] [Code Transformation Rule Synthesis using LLMs: Potential and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03592)
*Axel Allain,Aymeric Blot,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Due to their black-box nature, LLMs suffer from limited explain- ability and a lack of determinism. Their usage cost can also rise, particularly with repetitive tasks on large codebases. To mitigate this, we conduct a novel empirical study targeting three domain- specific languages for transformation rules, namely Comby, GritQL, and Ast-Grep. We evaluate three LLMs (GPT-5.4, GPT-oss-120B, and Llama3.1-8B) on six diverse datasets covering four software- evolution tasks: API misuse correction, program repair, API migra- tion, and language version migration. Our results provide evidence that transformation rule synthesis moves beyond proof-of-concept with strong frontier models. GPT-5.4 achieves consistently high rule applicability rates and produces transformations closest to the ground truth across most benchmarks. Smaller and open-weight GPT-oss-120B and Llama3.1-8B models remain effective for simpler, localized changes but struggle with complex migration scenarios. We also observe non-negligible generalizability through the usage of meta-variables and through a high reuse score in the first quartile of many datasets. Finally, when compared to the anti-unification algorithm, LLMs outperform it in correctness, but underperform in rule applicability. Overall, our results show great potential for LLMs to generate sound, correct, generalizable, and reusable rules.

</details>


### [95] [Can LLMs Extract Architectural Design Decisions from Source Code Commits? - A Preliminary Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03721)
*Amey Karan,Rudra Dhar,Mohamed Soliman,Karthik Vaidhyanathan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究初步探索了使用四种大语言模型（Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1、Kimi K2、Qwen3）从源代码提交中自动恢复架构设计决策（ADD）的有效性，通过零样本和少样本提示在30个开源项目的开发者撰写的ADD上进行评估，发现所有模型BERT-F1均超过0.81，少样本提示可提升对齐度，但生成的ADD往往过长、偏实现细节且缺乏决策背后的原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构设计决策（ADD）记录了软件系统结构和演进背后的原理，但很少被显式记录，通常隐藏于源代码提交中。恢复这些决策对架构知识管理（AKM）至关重要，然而从提交中提取ADD因其隐式和非结构化特性而具有挑战性。尽管LLM在理解代码和文本方面展现强大能力，但其在此任务上的效果尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 作者选取四种LLM（Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1、Kimi K2、Qwen3），采用零样本和少样本两种提示方式，在来自开源项目的30个开发者撰写的ADD上进行实验。使用ROUGE-L、BLEU、METEOR和BERTScore四种指标对输出进行自动评分，并由一位作者对Gemini输出进行人工审查。

Result: 所有模型BERT-F1均超过0.81，少样本提示改善了输出与参考的一致性（Gemini的BERT-F1从0.828提升至0.847）。但人工审查发现，生成的ADD往往过长、偏向实现细节描述，且遗漏了决策背后的原理性推理。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在从提交中恢复ADD方面展现了一定潜力，但距离实际应用仍有差距，尤其在捕获决策原理方面不足。这为开发具有架构感知能力的LLM系统以及推进自动化架构知识管理指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Context: Architectural Design Decisions (ADDs) capture the rationale behind the structure and evolution of software systems but are rarely documented explicitly, and are often hidden inside source code commits. Recovering them is important for Architectural Knowledge Management (AKM). Problem: Extracting ADDs from commits is challenging due to their implicit and unstructured nature. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in understanding code and text, yet their effectiveness for this task remains underexplored. Study: We present a preliminary study using four LLMs (Gemini 3 Pro, DeepSeek R1, Kimi K2, Qwen3) with zeroshot and fewshot prompting on 30 developer-written ADDs from open-source projects. We score outputs with ROUGE-L, BLEU, METEOR, and BERTScore, and one author manually reviews the Gemini outputs. Results: All models reach a BERT-F1 above 0.81, and fewshot prompting improves alignment (Gemini BERT-F1: 0.828 to 0.847). However, the generated ADDs are often too long, implementation-focused, and miss the rationale behind the decision. This highlights opportunities for architecture-aware LLM systems and automated AKM.

</details>


### [96] [No One Left Behind: Cross-Level Analysis for Sustainable Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03861)
*Masoum Salehi,Sandro Schulze,Jacob Krüger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"可持续性反模式"（sustainability antipatterns）这一新概念，用于捕捉软件工程中跨社会技术层面系统性产生不可持续结果的条件，并通过识别、形式化、检测和缓解这些反模式，为可持续软件工程建立综合且可操作的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件可持续性研究（如能效、资源优化、代码可维护性）缺乏对可持续性问题如何在社会技术各层面之间涌现并相互强化的解释。可持续性挑战并非孤立存在，而是源于组织优先级、开发实践与技术条件之间的相互作用，需要新的视角来揭示跨层面的错位。

Method: 这是一篇愿景论文（vision paper），提出了"可持续性反模式"的概念框架。该方法通过捕捉系统性产生不可持续结果的条件，来揭示软件工程中组织、流程和产品各层面之间以及不同可持续性维度之间难以通过面向制品或维度特定分析识别的错位。

Result: 论文论证了可持续性反模式概念的有效性，展示了它如何帮助暴露跨层面和跨可持续性维度的错位问题，并勾勒出未来研究的四条具体方向：识别、形式化、检测和缓解可持续性反模式。

Conclusion: 作者将可持续性反模式定位为可持续软件工程综合研究议程的基础，希望通过这一概念框架推动对软件工程可持续性问题的系统性理解和行动研究。

Abstract: Software engineers expect a software system to be efficient, maintainable, economically viable, and socially responsible throughout its lifecycle. Unfortunately, decisions made at the organizational, process, or product levels of software development - even when they improve one of these goals - often have unintended long-term consequences at other levels. In response, software-engineering research has explored different ways to achieve software sustainability, including energy efficiency, resource optimization, and code maintainability. However, existing research offers limited explanations for how sustainability problems emerge and reinforce one another across the socio-technical levels of software engineering. Moreover, we argue that sustainability challenges are not isolated but rather systemic: They stem from interactions among organizational priorities, development practices, and technical conditions. In this vision paper, we introduce the concept of sustainability antipatterns to capture conditions that systematically produce unsustainable outcomes. We demonstrate how this concept can help expose cross-level misalignments between socio-technical levels and sustainability dimensions that remain difficult to recognize through artifact-centric or dimension-specific analyses. To guide future research, we outline concrete directions for identifying, formalizing, detecting, and mitigating sustainability anti-patterns. We envision sustainability anti-patterns as a foundation for an integrated and actionable research agenda on sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [97] [LabelMate: An LLM-Driven Framework for Refined Issue Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04055)
*Liam Johnston,Shayan Noei,Maram Assi,Ying Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LabelMate是一个基于大型语言模型（LLM）的框架，用于自动为软件issue报告生成项目特定的标签集合并分配标签，无需预标注训练数据。在30个GitHub仓库的16,500个issue报告上实现了89.84%的平均标注准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件issue报告的标注对规划和社区参与很重要，但现有自动化标注方法存在手动干预多、标签过于通用、依赖已有标注数据集等局限性。

Method: 提出LabelMate框架，利用大型语言模型（LLM），从历史issue报告中推导出全面的、项目特定的标签集合，并自动为新issue报告分配合适的标签，无需预标注数据。

Result: 在来自30个热门且多样化的GitHub仓库的16,500个issue报告上评估，生成了275个连贯标签，平均标注准确率达89.84%，显著优于现有的通用标签分配方法。

Conclusion: LabelMate提供了一种高效、领域自适应的解决方案，能有效简化issue标注流程，克服现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Software users often submit issue reports to a product's issue tracking system to report defects, suggest enhancements, or raise other product-related concerns. Labeling these issue reports supports effective planning and improves community engagement. However, many issue reports remain unlabeled due to the substantial manual effort required to design an appropriate label taxonomy, then assign suitable labels from this taxonomy to new issue reports. Existing automated labeling approaches attempt to mitigate these challenges. However, they suffer from key limitations, such as extensive manual intervention, the assignment of generic labels, and a dependence on existing labeled datasets. To address these limitations, we propose LabelMate, a novel Large Language Model (LLM)-driven framework that (1) derives a comprehensive, project-specific label set from historical issue reports and (2) automatically assigns relevant labels to new issue reports without requiring any pre-labeled training data. We evaluate LabelMate on 16,500 issue reports from 30 popular and diverse GitHub repositories. Based on this dataset, our approach generates a coherent list of 275 labels and achieves an average labeling accuracy of 89.84%, a statistically significant improvement over existing generic label assigning approaches. These results demonstrate that LabelMate offers an efficient, domain-adaptive solution to streamline the issue labeling process.

</details>


### [98] [When Models Edit Too Much: On the Fidelity of Minimal Code Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04061)
*Tongyao Zhu,Wei Hern Lim,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在代码修复中的过度编辑问题，构建了基于400个BigCodeBench问题的评估框架，发现即使GPT-5.5等强模型也存在广泛的过度编辑行为。通过保持指令可将过度编辑降低约33%，同时提升Pass@1。训练方面，强化学习比监督微调在编辑保真度和性能保持之间取得更优的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码修复研究只关注正确性，忽略了修复应最小化改动、可审查且忠实于原始实现。过度编辑会引入不必要的认知复杂度，降低代码的可维护性，但缺乏系统性的评估方法和控制手段。

Method: 从BigCodeBench中选取400个问题，通过注入受控的AST级损坏构建已知最小补丁的修复任务，评估前沿LLM的过度编辑倾向。提出保持指令弱化行为，并对比监督微调与强化学习对编辑保真度的学习效果。

Result: 过度编辑在高性能模型中普遍存在；保持指令将平均超额编辑距离从0.195降至0.131，认知复杂度降低26.6%，Pass@1提升2.3个百分点。但这种提升并非来自更多推理预算或更大模型。监督微调过拟合已见损坏模式，强化学习在领域外编辑保真度和性能保持上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 编辑保真度是代码修复质量的一个独立维度，可以被量化度量且可通过训练习得。这是对仅以正确性为目标的代码修复评估的重要补充。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to edit existing code, but correctness alone is not enough: useful repairs should also be minimal, reviewable, and faithful to the original implementation. We study over-editing, the tendency of a model to rewrite code beyond what is required to fix a bug. We construct an evaluation framework from 400 BigCodeBench problems by injecting controlled AST-level corruptions into reference solutions, giving each repair task a known minimal patch. Across frontier LLMs, over-editing is widespread even among strong models like GPT-5.5: high Pass@1 can coexist with unnecessarily large edits and added cognitive complexity. A preservation instruction substantially reduces this behavior, lowering average excess Levenshtein distance from 0.195 to 0.131, reducing added cognitive complexity by 26.6%, and increasing Pass@1 by 2.3 points. However, these gains do not simply follow from a larger reasoning budget or larger models. We next ask whether minimal editing can be learned directly during post-training. We observe that supervised fine-tuning overfits to seen corruption patterns, whereas reinforcement learning gives the best out-of-domain edit-fidelity and performance-retention trade-off. These results position edit fidelity as a distinct axis of code-repair quality and show that it can be measured and learned.

</details>


### [99] [ATIBA: Grounded Integrity and Quality Checking for Research Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04123)
*Veli Karakaya,Semih Çağlar,Yusuf Yiğit Korkmaz,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATIBA是一个论文质量控制工具，对稿件执行五项基于"可验证证据"的完整性检查（引用完整性、会议合规性、经验标准合规、多模式AI评审和引文推荐）。其核心设计原则是：LLM只被信任用于判断，绝不用于编造其判断所依据的证据。经过13名非作者参与者的用户研究，六个调查项的同意率在69%-92%之间（均值85%），初步证明了感知有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检查稿件的引用完整性、对目标会议特定提交规则的合规性以及社区报告标准的遵循情况，目前是手动、重复且因会议而异的工作，实践中往往执行不一致或被跳过，缺乏自动化工具支持。

Method: 开发了ATIBA工具，实现五项"接地"（grounded）的完整性和质量检查：引用完整性检查（对照书目来源验证引用并标记撤稿/不可查找的文献）、会议/轨道合规检查（直接从会议征稿启事页面推导提交标准并附带原文引用）、经验标准合规检查（对照ACM SIGSOFT经验标准，含幻觉防御机制，丢弃无法在稿中原样找到的证据引用）、基于GPT-5.4的多模式AI评审（会议特定、正式和页面锚定注释）、引文建议功能（先对照书目来源验证再展示给用户）。通过13名非作者参与者的主持式用户研究评估。

Result: 用户研究中六个调查项的同意率范围为69%-92%，平均85%，为各评估流程中的积极感知有用性提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: ATIBA的五个检查均围绕同一原则设计：LLM只能做判断，不能编造判断依据的证据。研究建立了感知有用性；客观准确性仍需进一步测量。

Abstract: Checking a manuscript's reference integrity, its compliance with a target venue's specific submission rules, and its adherence to community reporting standards is manual, repetitive, and different for every venue so in practice it is done inconsistently or skipped. We present ATIBA, a tool that runs five grounded integrity and quality checks on a manuscript: a reference-integrity check that verifies each citation against bibliographic sources and flags retracted or unfindable references; a venue/track compliance check that derives submission criteria directly from a venue's own call-for-papers page and evaluates the manuscript against them, each verdict anchored to a verbatim quote from that page; an empirical-standards compliance check against the ACM SIGSOFT Empirical Standards, with a hallucination defence that discards any evidence quote it cannot locate verbatim in the manuscript; a multi-mode AI review (venue-specific, formal, and page-anchored annotation) powered by GPT-5.4 through Azure OpenAI; and a citation-suggestion feature that proposes candidate references for a manuscript and verifies each against bibliographic sources before it is shown to the user. All five checks are designed around the same principle: an LLM is only trusted to judge, never to invent the evidence it judges against. We evaluated ATIBA through a moderated user study with 13 non-author participants. Agreement across the six survey items ranged from 69% to 92%, with a mean of 85%, providing initial evidence of positive perceived usefulness across the evaluated workflows. These findings establish perceived usefulness; objective accuracy remains to be measured.

</details>


### [100] [SWE-Gate: Passing Functional Tests Is Not Enough for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04167)
*Xin He,Yanlin Wang,Mingwei Liu,Jiachi Chen,Hongyu Zhang,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-Gate，一个首个显式评估代码智能体对评审约束（review constraints）合规性的仓库级软件工程基准。基于303个真实PR评审评论衍生的修复实例，实验发现644个通过功能测试的补丁中有221个不满足评审约束，表明仅依据功能测试的评估会高估智能体的真实能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级软件工程基准仅通过功能测试衡量补丁是否通过，忽略了真实软件开发中评审意见衍生的验收约束（review constraints）对补丁可接受性的重要影响，导致评估结果不能反映实际开发场景中的完整需求。

Method: 从真实PR评审评论中提取评审约束，并围绕这些约束合成仓库级修复实例。每个实例包含独立的功能测试和约束测试，以及不合规补丁和黄金补丁，从而将问题解决能力与评审约束合规能力显式分离。构建了覆盖75个开源Python仓库的303个实例，并在统一编码智能体框架下使用四个不同能力层级的LLM后端进行实验。

Result: 在644个通过功能测试的修复中，有221个未能满足所提供的评审约束，揭示了功能成功与完整修复规范成功之间存在显著差距，证明仅基于功能的评估会高估智能体完成仓库级修复任务完整需求的能力。

Conclusion: 代码智能体在真实仓库级修复中不仅需要功能正确，还需满足评审约束。SWE-Gate为更全面地评估编码智能体提供了新基准，暴露了现有评估范式的局限性。

Abstract: Repository-level software engineering benchmarks have significantly advanced the evaluation of coding agents, but existing benchmarks primarily measure whether generated patches pass functional tests and overlook review-derived acceptance constraints (review constraints) that often influence whether a patch is acceptable in real-world software development. We introduce SWE-Gate, a repository-level benchmark for software engineering agents that explicitly evaluates review constraint compliance alongside functional correctness. SWE-Gate derives review constraints from real pull request review comments and synthesizes repository-level repair instances around these constraints. Each instance provides separate functional and constraint tests, together with non-compliant and gold patches, enabling explicit separation between issue resolution capability and review constraint compliance. We construct SWE-Gate with 303 repository-level repair instances spanning 75 open-source Python repositories across diverse software domains. Experiments with four LLM backends spanning different capability levels under a common coding-agent scaffold reveal a substantial gap between functional success and success under the complete repair specification: among 644 repairs that pass the functional tests, 221 fail to satisfy the provided review constraints. These findings show that functional-only evaluation overestimates agents' ability to satisfy the full requirements of repository-level repair tasks. The replication package including code, data, and experimental results is available at https://github.com/DeepSoftwareAnalytics/SWE-Gate.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Practical Threshold-based Tree Edit Distance Lower-Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03078)
*Lukáš Moravec,Radim Bača*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文全面比较了基于树编辑距离（TED）的相似性搜索中的各种下界方法，提出了加速SED下界的Ukkonen算法变体，并引入结合结构约束的SED-struct过滤器，在合成和真实数据集上均实现了最高过滤精度且保持实用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于TED的树状数据相似性搜索计算代价高昂，现有下界方法存在权衡困境：便宜的统计/结构下界剪枝能力弱，而精确的基于遍历的字符串编辑距离（SED）下界因标准二次动态规划而计算昂贵。此外，以往比较研究未涵盖最新的结构过滤器及阈值感知的SED实现，导致其实用性能不明确。

Method: 首先对所有先进的TED下界进行了全面的实验对比（评估剪枝精度与计算成本）；其次利用Ukkonen有界字符串编辑距离算法加速SED下界计算（不影响剪枝能力的前提下大幅降低运行时间）；最后提出SED-struct阈值过滤器，将SED与轴感知（axes-aware）约束相结合，利用树节点的结构关系增强过滤能力。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的实验表明，SED-struct在所有方法中持续获得最高过滤精度，同时保持实用的过滤成本；尤其对于异构树集合（标准SED下界精度较低的场景）效果显著。

Conclusion: SED-struct作为一种融合结构约束的增强型下界过滤器，在过滤精度与计算成本之间实现了优越的平衡，特别适用于异构树集合的TED相似性检索场景。

Abstract: Threshold-based similarity search over tree-structured data using tree edit distance (TED) is computationally intensive. Given a query tree and a database of trees, the goal is to retrieve all trees within a predefined TED threshold $τ$. Because exact TED computation is expensive, practical methods employ lower-bounds to prune dissimilar candidates before verification. Existing lower-bounds exhibit a fundamental trade-off: inexpensive statistical and structural bounds provide limited pruning power, whereas the more precise traversal-based string edit distance (SED) bound is expensive to compute using standard quadratic dynamic programming. Moreover, previous comparative studies do not cover recent structural filters or threshold-aware SED implementations, leaving their practical trade-offs unclear. In this article, we first provide a comprehensive experimental comparison of state-of-the-art TED lower-bounds in terms of pruning precision and computational cost. We then accelerate the SED lower-bound using Ukkonen's bounded string edit distance algorithm, substantially reducing its runtime without affecting its pruning power. Finally, we introduce the SED-struct threshold filter, which strengthens SED with axes-aware constraints capturing structural relationships among tree nodes. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that SED-struct consistently achieves the highest filtering precision while retaining practical filtering costs. The results suggest that SED-struct is particularly beneficial for heterogeneous tree collections in which the standard SED lower-bound achieves relatively low precision.

</details>


### [102] [What Happens When the Model Eats the Stack? Rethinking the Research Agenda for Data Agents to Withstand the Bitter Lesson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03141)
*Liana Patel,Siddharth Jha,Negar Arabzadeh,Carlos Guestrin,Ion Stoica,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文基于"苦涩的教训"视角，认为随着LLM能力的不断提升，许多为弥补模型局限而设计的系统层将逐渐被模型本身所取代。作者主张数据系统领域应转向支持跨多次查询的"持久语义上下文"（persistent semantic context）这一核心抽象，并指出了相关的系统研究挑战与机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM端到端训练正在快速吸收原本需要精心工程化数据代理才能实现的能力，这给数据系统社区带来了根本性挑战——传统上为补偿模型缺陷而构建的系统层可能很快变得多余。

Method: 通过经验性洞察论证系统层将被模型子sumed的趋势，提出了"持久语义上下文"这一核心概念——即跨多次查询向数据代理提供关于数据环境的精选上下文信息，并探讨将其作为一流抽象原生服务所需的系统设计方向。

Result: 研究发现上下文层能显著提升数据代理性能，但也引入重大系统挑战，包括高效上下文数据结构设计、存储方法、压缩技术以及语义一致性协议等方面。

Conclusion: 未来数据系统的关键要求在于将持久语义上下文作为一等公民抽象进行原生服务，以此赋能能够处理大规模复杂知识语料的数据代理，并为该愿景指出了若干值得探索的新研究方向。

Abstract: The bitter lesson poses an existential question for the data systems community, whereby large language models (LLMs) trained end-to-end are rapidly internalizing new capabilities that previously required carefully engineered data agents. Guided by empirical insights, we argue that as models continue to improve, many proposed system layers designed to compensate for model limitations on a given task will increasingly be subsumed by the model itself. We instead identify enduring research opportunities, which lie in supporting data agents across many queries with curated contextual information about the data environment, which we call persistent semantic context. We find that these context layers demonstrate strong promise for improving data agent performance, but they also raise significant system challenges. Thus, a key requirement for future data systems will lie in natively serving persistent semantic contexts as a first-class abstraction in order to enable capable data agents working over huge, complex knowledge corpora. Towards this vision, we outline exciting new research opportunities, including designing efficient context data structures, storage methods, compression techniques, and semantic consistency protocols, to ensure integrity and correctness of the stored contextual knowledge.

</details>


### [103] [Does SRL Pave the Road to Explainable Reasoning? Lessons Learned from an Implementer's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03441)
*Lander Maes,Bryan-Elliott Tam,Jitse De Smet,Jos De Roo,Pieter Colpaert,Ruben Taelman*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文实现了两个Shape Rules Language (SRL)推理引擎——一个基于现有SPARQL引擎复用其解析器和CONSTRUCT功能，另一个为专用引擎。专用引擎速度是SPARQL引擎的2至6倍，且差距随规则集增大而扩大。两者均通过SRL一致性测试套件验证，表明即便SRL规范尚不成熟，该语言已能支持实用的推理任务，且基于SPARQL构建SRL引擎成本低廉，仅需接受中等程度的速度折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SRL作为定义RDF推理规则的工作草案，缺乏权威实现，导致实践者对如何构建合规引擎以及该语言能解决哪些问题缺乏指导。

Method: 实现两个SRL引擎：一个复用现有SPARQL查询引擎及其模块化解析器，使用CONSTRUCT生成三元组；另一个构建专用引擎。两者均在经典RDF推理任务上评估正确性、完整性和速度，并通过SRL一致性测试套件及额外用例测试验证。

Result: 专用引擎比SPARQL引擎快2至6倍，性能差距随规则集规模增大而扩大；两个引擎均通过一致性测试，验证了各自实现的正确性。

Conclusion: 在SPARQL引擎之上构建可用的SRL引擎成本低廉，虽然存在中等速度折中，但可由专用实现弥补。SRL规范虽不成熟，但已支持实际有用的推理任务。

Abstract: The Shape Rules Language (SRL) Working Draft defines how to derive new RDF triples from an RDF graph using inference rules. Each rule matches graph patterns and instantiates triple templates whose output feeds into validation pipelines, SPARQL queries, or further inference. RDF reasoning has traditionally relied on fixed entailment regimes (RDFS, OWL), rule-based ad-hoc languages such as N3, or other implementation-specific solutions without a shared standard. SRL introduces user-defined production rules with a defined grammar, dependency analysis, execution ordering, and termination guarantees. However, no authoritative implementation exists, leaving practitioners with little guidance on how to build a conformant engine or on what problems the language can solve. We implemented two SRL engines and evaluated both on classical RDF reasoning tasks for soundness, completeness, and speed. The first reuses an existing SPARQL query engine and its query parser; the second is a dedicated engine. The SPARQL-based engine reused an existing modular parser for query construction and SPARQL CONSTRUCT for triple production, reducing engine-specific work. The dedicated engine was two to six times faster, the gap widening as rule sets grow. Both engines were validated against the SRL conformance test suite, supplemented by additional use-case-driven tests. A usable SRL engine can be built inexpensively on top of a SPARQL engine, with a moderate speed trade-off that a dedicated implementation recovers. Despite the specification's immaturity, the language already supports practically useful reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [104] [KnowFeat: Knowledge-Guided Feature Engineering via LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03529)
*Chengsong You,Wangyue Li,Weiqiao Que,Qizhou Chen,Kunyan Wu,Wei Deng,Feng Zhu,Xiaofeng He*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出KnowFeat框架，将领域知识组织为五类结构化上下文注入LLM智能体，用于表格数据的自动化特征工程，并通过三阶段验证管线筛选候选特征。在十二个公开基准上排名第一（平均排名2.3），优于七种对比方法（p=0.017），在电信流失数据集上AUC最高提升11.6个百分点，在AML数据集上兼具竞争性检测性能和完整的可溯源能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化特征工程方法缺乏结构化领域知识注入、严格的验证机制以及可解释的特征来源追溯，导致生成的特征虽语义丰富但可靠性不足，难以在实际应用中落地。

Method: 提出KnowFeat框架：(1) 将领域知识分类为五类——模式元数据、监管指标、检测规则、专家意见和法庭文档证据，并作为结构化上下文注入LLM智能体；(2) 构建三阶段验证管线，依次通过代码执行、统计质量检查和模型有效性评估来筛选候选特征；(3) 每个被接受的特征都附带溯源记录，将特征设计追溯到特定的知识资产；(4) 采用严格的留出协议以避免特征选择泄漏。
