Result: 在十二个公共基准上，KnowFeat在七种方法中平均排名第一（平均排名2.3，单侧Wilcoxon p=0.017），在电信流失数据集上AUC峰值提升达+11.6个百分点；在真实比特币反洗钱数据集（Elliptic）和合成数字货币反洗钱基准（SimECNY）上，KeepFeat在保持竞争性检测性能的同时实现了完整的溯源可追踪性。

Conclusion: 将结构化领域知识与LLM智能体相结合，配合严格的验证管线和溯源机制，能够显著提升自动化特征工程的有效性、可靠性与可解释性，尤其适用于反洗钱等需要合规性与可追溯性的高风险领域。

Abstract: Automated feature engineering with large language models (LLMs) can produce semantically meaningful features for tabular data, yet existing methods lack structured domain knowledge, rigorous verification, and explainable provenance. We propose KnowFeat, a knowledge-guided feature engineering framework that organizes domain knowledge into five types -- schema metadata, regulatory indicators, detection rules, expert opinions, and court document evidence -- and injects them as structured context into an LLM agent. A three-stage verification pipeline filters candidates through code execution, statistical quality checks, and model effectiveness evaluation. Every accepted feature carries a provenance record tracing its design to specific knowledge assets. Under a strict held-out protocol that eliminates feature-selection leakage, KnowFeat ranks first (avg. rank 2.3) across twelve public benchmarks among seven methods (one-sided Wilcoxon p=0.017), with a peak gain of +11.6 pp AUC on a telecom churn dataset. On a real-world Bitcoin anti-money laundering (AML) dataset (Elliptic) and a synthetic digital currency AML benchmark (SimECNY), KnowFeat maintains competitive detection performance with full provenance traceability.

</details>


### [105] [Tree Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03538)
*Nicolas Spyratos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以带标签的有向树为基础结构的树数据库模型，树根代表对象、非根节点代表属性、根到属性的路径表示语义。该模型通过函数代数（限制、组合、配对和笛卡尔积）统一定义了树的运算和查询语言，包含遍历查询与分析查询两类，并将后者建立在前者之上，实现了数据访问与数据分析在同一形式框架内统一，与关系模型形成鲜明对比。论文展示了该模型在继承支持、构建一致性关系数据库以及作为关系数据用户友好接口等方面的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系模型中分析查询（如SQL Group-by）在关系代数之外单独定义，缺乏统一的形式框架来同时支持数据访问与数据分析，因此作者希望构建一个两者都处于同一形式框架内的数据库模型。

Method: 定义带标签有向树的树数据库模型（树集合），通过函数代数（函数限制、函数复合、函数配对和集合笛卡尔积四个操作）定义树的操作与查询语言；查询分为遍历查询与分析查询，其中遍历查询是分析查询的构建基础。

Result: 通过三个方面的演示证明了模型的表达能力：(a)无缝支持继承机制；(b)可在树数据库之上定义一致性关系数据库，使树数据库充当底层语义层；(c)树数据库可作为访问和分析关系数据的用户友好接口。

Conclusion: 该模型以单一函数代数统一了树操作与查询语言（遍历与分析），克服了关系模型中数据访问与数据分析分离的问题，并展现出良好的表达能力与应用灵活性。

Abstract: We propose a novel database model whose basic structure is a labeled, directed tree with node identities. Intuitively, the root of the tree is seen as an object (or entity), the non-root nodes as attributes of the object and the semantics of each attribute is represented by the unique path leading from the root to the attribute. We define a tree database to be a set of such trees. The trees of the database can be combined to produce new trees using a set of operations on trees that we define in the paper. The query language of our model offers two types of queries, traversal queries and analytic queries. A query (whether traversal or analytic) is always defined over a tree, which is either a tree in the database or a tree derived from other trees using tree operations. The operations on trees and the query language are both defined using a simple functional algebra whose operations are: restriction of a function, composition of functions, pairing of functions and Cartesian product of sets.
  A distinctive feature of our model is that traversal queries and analytic queries are both defined within the same formal framework; and in fact, traversal queries serve as the building blocks for analytic queries. This is in sharp contrast to the relational model, where analytic queries are defined outside the relational algebra, in the form of SQL Group-by queries. Therefore our model supports data access and data analysis within the same formal framework.
  We demonstrate the expressive power of our model by showing: (a) how our model can support inheritance in a seamless manner, (b) how one can define consistent relational databases on top of a tree database - with the tree database playing the role of an underlying semantic layer and (c) how a tree database can be used as a user-friendly interface for accessing and analyzing relational data.

</details>


### [106] [Convolution Sum-Product Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03672)
*Kyle Deeds,Timo Camillo Merkl,Dan Suciu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了卷积和-积查询（CSPQs）的求值问题，CSPQs是允许原子包含变量线性组合（如$A(2X+3Y-Z, Y+Z)$）的SPQ扩展。作者利用线性代数技术（秩分析、商空间、变量替换）设计了多种求值算法，包括将最坏情况最优连接算法适配到CSPQs，以及扩展树分解定义以实现因子化执行，并定义了三个宽度度量来刻画复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSPQs在图像处理等实际场景以及$(\min,+)$-卷积和$k$-SUM猜想等理论场景中都有应用，且能捕获带线性等式约束的SPQ。然而，以往针对线性和不等式约束的研究方法在CSPQs场景下渐近次优，因此需要专门针对CSPQs的高效求值算法。

Method: 作者提出两种主要算法框架：（1）将Worst-Case Optimal Joins适配到CSPQs，利用线性代数技术如秩分析、商空间和变量替换；（2）扩展树分解（TD）定义至CSPQs，使线性组合成为一等公民，在分解中扮演与传统TD中变量相同的角色，并定义了三个宽度度量以限定执行复杂度。方法还被推广到有理数之外的任意域。

Result: 实现了对CSPQs的Worst-Case Optimal Join执行，复杂度在精神上与合取查询的WCOJ类似；通过扩展的树分解实现因子化执行，并基于域值大小、活动域大小和关系支持度定义三个宽度度量来界定复杂度界限。

Conclusion: 本文填补了CSPQs求值的空白，提供了优于先前线性约束设置下技术的求值算法，并将方法推广至任意域，为CSPQs的系统性高效求值奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study query evaluation for an extension of sum-product queries (SPQ) that allows atoms with linear combinations of variables (e.g. $A(2X+3Y-Z, Y+Z)$), which we call convolution sum-product queries (CSPQs). These queries arise in both practical settings (e.g. image processing workloads) and theoretical ones (e.g. the $(\min,+)$-convolution and $k$-SUM conjectures), and capture SPQs with added linear equality constraints. While prior work has considered the impact of linear in- and dis-equality constraints on query evaluation, the techniques developed in that setting are asymptotically sub-optimal for CSPQs. To address this, we describe several evaluation algorithms for CSPQs that leverage linear algebra techniques like rank analysis, quotient spaces, and variable substitution. First, we adapt Worst-Case Optimal Joins to CSPQs, achieving a runtime similar in spirit to that for conjunctive queries. Then, we extend the definition of tree decompositions (TDs) to CSPQs, and describe a factorized execution. In this extension, linear combinations are first-class citizens and play the same role as variables in traditional TDs. We define three width measures that bound the complexity of this execution with respect to the size of the domain values, of the active domain, and of the relation's support. Lastly, we show how these methods can be applied to arbitrary fields beyond the rationals.

</details>


### [107] [Aker: Density-Aware Approximate Caching for Vector Search (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03712)
*Sukjoon Oh,Minki Kang,Dohyun Kim,Baotong Lu,Jing Liu,Qianxi Zhang,Qi Chen,Youjip Won*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Aker是一个面向磁盘型近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）的近似缓存系统，通过引入逐查询自适应相似度阈值和del-consistency一致性模型，分别解决现有方法在命中谓词精度不足和缓存刷新机制缺失两方面的局限，在pgvector上集成后显著提升了召回率和QPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁盘型ANNS在索引遍历过程中因频繁磁盘访问产生高昂I/O开销。近似缓存通过复用历史查询结果来服务相似查询，有望绕过昂贵的磁盘搜索。然而现有方法存在两大局限：一是近似命中谓词无法在高维空间中同时兼顾高吞吐和高精度，无法适应局部邻域密度的变化；二是缺乏在向量更新下维护缓存正确性的有效刷新机制。

Method: Aker提出两个核心设计：(1) 逐查询相似度阈值——每个缓存条目维护自身动态调整的阈值，基于观测到的缓存命中模式自适应调整，以适应邻域密度变化，兼顾效率与精度；(2) del-consistency一致性模型——对删除操作采用急切（eager）应用，对插入操作采用惰性（lazy）应用，在该模型下实现低开销的刷新机制，以约束缓存陈旧性并保持高搜索精度。系统集成到pgvector中进行评估。

Result: 在代表性工作负载上的评估显示：相比现有方案，Aker的召回率最高提升64个百分点，QPS最高提升3.2倍，同时内存占用仅为pgvector共享缓冲区的0.6倍。

Conclusion: Aker通过自适应逐查询阈值和del-consistency一致性模型，有效解决了磁盘型ANNS缓存中精度-效率权衡以及向量更新下的缓存一致性问题，在显著提升吞吐量的同时保持了高精度，且内存开销更低。

Abstract: Disk-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) incurs high I/O overhead due to frequent disk accesses during index traversal. Approximate caching, which reuses the results of past queries to serve future similar queries, offers a promising approach to bypass expensive disk searches. However, existing approaches suffer from two key limitations. First, their approximate hit predicates fail to simultaneously achieve high throughput and high accuracy, as they do not adapt to the varying local neighbor density in high-dimensional spaces. Second, they lack an effective refresh mechanism to maintain cache correctness under vector updates.
  We present Aker, an approximate cache for disk-based ANNS. Aker addresses these limitations through two core design choices. First, we introduce a per-query similarity threshold, where each cache entry maintains its own threshold that is dynamically adjusted based on observed cache hit patterns. This design enables Aker to adapt to neighborhood densities to preserve both efficiency and accuracy. Second, we propose del-consistency, a consistency model for ANNS caches that applies deletions eagerly and insertions lazily. Under this model, Aker implements a low-overhead refresh mechanism that bounds cache staleness and preserves high search accuracy. We integrate Aker into pgvector and evaluate it on representative workloads. Aker improves recall by up to 64 percentage points over prior solutions and increases QPS by up to 3.2x, while using 0.6x the memory of pgvector's shared buffers.

</details>


### [108] [From Data Querying to Data Investigations: Rethinking Natural Language Interfaces for Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03898)
*Fabian Wenz,Zixuan Chen,Carsten Binnig*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文指出现有的自然语言数据库接口（Text-to-SQL）范式存在根本性问题，即只假设用户提出可用单条SQL查询回答的问题，而实际用户需要的是"数据调查"——通过多步SQL查询序列搜索数据库并对中间结果进行推理来解决问题。作者提出新范式"data investigations"，并构建了原型系统D²，该系统能自主搜索、推理和收集数据来解决数据问题，并在基于Murder Mystery数据集的新基准上展示了其潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Text-to-SQL范式被错误地优化了。实际用户寻求的是解决数据问题的帮助，这需要按序列执行多个SQL查询并在中间结果上进行推理，而非仅运行单个SQL查询。作者认为当前范式与实际需求之间存在根本性错配。

Method: 提出了"数据调查"（data investigations）这一新范式，并开发了原型系统D²，该系统能够自主搜索、推理并收集数据以解决复杂数据问题。同时构建了基于Murder Mystery数据集的新基准来评估系统性能。

Result: 在基于Murder Mystery数据集的新基准上，D²在需要数据调查和证据驱动决策的任务上展示了其能力，超越了传统单查询问答系统的表现边界。

Conclusion: 数据调查是自然语言数据库接口的一个更有价值的新范式，D²作为首个原型证明了这一方向的可行性，为未来NL-to-data系统的发展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Natural language (NL) interfaces to databases have been optimized for the wrong problem. The dominant Text-to-SQL paradigm assumes that users ask questions that can be answered by single SQL queries. In practice, however, users seek assistance with solving data problems. This requires searching a database by sequences of SQL queries while reasoning over intermediate results instead of just running one SQL query. This paper therefore introduces a new paradigm for NL interfaces to data, which we call data investigations. We present D^2, a first prototype of a data investigation system that embodies this vision by autonomously searching, reasoning over, and collecting data to solve data problems. Using a newly constructed benchmark based on the Murder Mystery dataset, we demonstrate the potential of D^2 for tasks that require data investigations with evidence-backed decisions, extending beyond the capabilities of traditional single-query question answering.

</details>


### [109] [Property Testing for Recursive Query Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03908)
*Isolde Adler,Carsten Lutz,Quentin Manière,Marcin Przybyłko,Lukas Schulze*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究数据库查询语境下的递归查询语言性质测试问题，重点关注双向正则路径查询（2RPQ）和一元Datalog程序。主要结果包括：任何2RPQ的非答案可用常数次查询单侧错误测试；线性递归且规则体为α-无环的一元Datalog程序也可常数查询测试；但若非等价于α-无环程序的一元Datalog程序（规则体无自连接）则不可常数查询单侧错误测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据库查询领域，性质测试为仅检查数据库亚线性部分即可高置信度测试查询答案提供了框架。已有的基础结果是关于布尔合取查询的（Chen和Yoshida 2019），但对于递归查询语言（如2RPQ和一元Datalog）的性质测试此前尚未被研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文通过扩展Chen和Yoshida关于布尔合取查询的性质测试框架，对递归查询语言进行分类研究。首先证明所有2RPQ的非答案可常数查询测试；然后将其推广到线性递归且规则体为α-无环的一元Datalog程序；最后对一般性一元Datalog程序给出不可能性结果，并在α-无环规则体但非线性递归的特殊情形下给出观察。

Result: 主要正面结果：任何2RPQ的非答案具有常数查询单侧错误可测试性，且可扩展到一定类别的一元Datalog程序（线性递归+α-无环规则体）。主要负面结果：若一元Datalog程序Π不等价于α-无环程序（在规则体无自连接的假设下），则Π的假性不可用常数查询单侧错误测试。

Conclusion: 本文系统地开启了递归查询语言（2RPQ和一元Datalog）性质测试的研究。2RPQ与α-无环限制下的线性递归一元Datalog均有正面结果，而一般一元Datalog在无自连接条件下存在不可能性。α-无环规则体但非线性递归的情形仍为开放问题，但已观察到某些轻微非线性程序可常数查询测试。

Abstract: In the context of database querying, property testing provides a framework for testing query answers with high confidence while inspecting only a sublinear part of the database, through completion queries and size queries. A fundamental result of Chen and Yoshida (2019) states that non-satisfaction of a Boolean conjunctive query $q$ is testable with a constant number of such queries and one-sided error if and only if $q$ is equivalent to an $α$-acyclic query. In this article, we initiate the study of property testing for recursive query languages, focusing on two-way regular path queries (2RPQs) and monadic Datalog. One of our main results is positive: non-answers to any 2RPQ are constant query testable with one-sided error. We extend this slightly to a certain class of monadic Datalog programs in which recursion is restricted to be linear and rule bodies must be $α$-acyclic. Turning towards unrestricted monadic Datalog, we next show that if a monadic Datalog program $Π$ is not equivalent to an $α$-acyclic program, then falsity of $Π$ is not constant query testable with one-sided error. This is under the assumption that all rule-bodies are self-join free. We leave open the case of monadic Datalog programs with $α$-acyclic rule bodies that are not restricted to linear recursion, but observe as a first step that there exist $α$-acyclic programs that are mildly non-linear and constant query testable with one-sided error.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [CHSR-RRF: A curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework with reciprocal rank fusion and leakage-aware benchmarking for educational RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02913)
*Terence Ateya,Zavier Ndum Ndum,Jicheng Fu,Kelly Tendongkeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CHSR-RRF框架，通过在检索前施加课程元数据约束来解决教育场景中的"课程泄漏"问题，并引入CERB基准数据集验证效果。实验表明前置门控可将泄漏降低4.6倍且不损失召回率，而后置约束则导致性能完全崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的RAG检索器只优化主题相关性，而不考虑课程有效性。在教育场景中，检索到的段落可能内容相关但与学科、学段或考试情境不符，这种"课程泄漏"是现有方法忽略的关键问题。

Method: 提出CHSR-RRF框架，核心思路是在检索之前施加元数据约束构建候选池，再结合稀疏检索和稠密检索进行倒数排名融合以及确定性重排序。同时构建CERB基准，包含126个案例，带有层级感知的相关性标注和显式泄漏注释。

Result: 在61例试验中，检索前门控使泄漏降低4.6倍（p<0.001）同时保持排名召回；而检索后施加相同约束会导致召回率和精确范围成功率为零（p=0.039）。完整基准的下界分析显示，剩余失败主要源于语料库和元数据缺口。

Conclusion: 在结构化教育领域的检索应被视为约束选择问题，有效性校验应在候选池形成阶段而非排序之后执行。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used in educational question answering, but standard retrievers optimize topical relevance without enforcing curriculum validity. In school settings, a passage can be relevant yet inappropriate if it comes from the wrong subject, level, or examination context; we call this failure mode curriculum leakage. We present CHSR-RRF, a curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework that applies metadata constraints before retrieval, then combines sparse and dense search with reciprocal rank fusion and deterministic reranking. We also introduce CERB, a 126-case benchmark for curriculum-constrained retrieval with hierarchy-aware relevance labels and explicit leakage annotations. On a 61-case pilot, pre-retrieval gating reduces leakage by 4.6x ($p<0.001$) while preserving ranked recall, whereas applying the same constraints after retrieval collapses recall and exact-scope success to zero ($p=0.039$). A full-benchmark lower-bound analysis further shows that many remaining failures arise from corpus and metadata gaps rather than retrieval design alone. These results show that retrieval in structured educational domains should be treated as constrained selection, with validity enforced when the candidate pool is formed rather than after ranking.

</details>


### [111] [Reflect-SQL: A Self-Reflection Based Framework for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02944)
*Anupreksha Jain,Manish Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Reflect-SQL框架，通过多阶段自我反思方法解决Text-to-SQL在实际企业应用中的三大挑战：复杂模糊的数据库模式、低效的表列检索、以及缺乏验证纠正机制的SQL生成问题。框架整合LLM-as-a-judge评分机制与多个互联反馈循环，在BIRD基准上达到72.03%的执行准确率，显著超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Text-to-SQL对现代企业的数据民主化至关重要，但实际部署受到现实复杂性的严重阻碍，包括数据库模式模糊且庞大、结构化模式下的表列检索效率低下、以及缺乏稳健的SQL验证与纠正机制导致生成有语法或逻辑缺陷的SQL。

Method: Reflect-SQL采用多阶段自我反思方法：1）利用知识库理解模糊数据库模式；2）建立有效的检索流程；3）构建SQL语法/语义生成系统。核心机制包括：LLM-as-a-judge驱动的评分机制嵌入互联反馈循环中，#retrieval loop迭代精化用户自然语言查询，synthesis loop验证并纠正SQL，entailment loop优化端到端流程并持续丰富知识库。

Result: 在具有挑战性的BIRD基准上，Reflect-SQL实现了72.03%的执行准确率，显著优于现有最先进基线方法，展示了企业级应用可靠性的重大提升。

Conclusion: 通过整合多层自我反思机制，Reflect-SQL有效弥合了用户意图与复杂数据之间的关键鸿沟，为Text-to-SQL在企业真实场景中的实用化部署提供了可靠的技术路径。

Abstract: Democratizing data access through natural language is a crucial goal for modern enterprises, but the practical adoption of Text-to-SQL is critically hindered by real-world complexities: 1. Obscure and large database schemas, 2. Ineffective retrieval of relevant tables and columns due to structured setting of schemas and vague user query, 3. Generation of syntactically or logically flawed SQL due to a lack of robust validation and correction mechanism. To address these systemic challenges, we introduce Reflect-SQL, a novel framework for Text to SQL, grounded in multi-stage self-reflection approach to develop understanding of obscure schema using a knowledge base, setup a process for effective retrieval and system to generate syntactically/semantically SQL. Instead of a single-pass attempt, our system employs an LLM-as-a-judge driven scoring mechanism within interconnected feedback loops to iteratively refine the results at every stage. A feedback-driven retrieval loop refines the user's natural language query, while a synthesis loop validates and corrects the SQL and finally, an entailment loop optimizes the end-to-end process and continuously enriches the knowledge base. By integrating these layers of reflection, Reflect-SQL bridges the critical gap between user intent and complex data. On the challenging BIRD benchmark, our framework achieves an execution accuracy of 72.03%, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating a major leap in reliability for enterprise applications.

</details>


### [112] [UniCon: A Unified Context-Centric Modeling Paradigm for CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03290)
*Jiajun Cui,Zhengqi Xu,Fan Zhang,Zhangteng,Gu Tang,Honghong Zhu,Mengxi Wu,Yulin Liang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniCon是一种统一的上下文中心建模架构，将请求上下文视为基本建模单元，将历史和预测目标组织为同质上下文单元，通过上下文内注意力建模局部耦合（Locality）和上下文间注意力建模决策状态的动态演化（Dynamics），有效弥合了历史和目标之间的结构差距，在美团搜索广告上显著提升离线AUC和在线RPM、CTR及收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业CTR预测的统一建模方法通常在token级别统一序列和非序列信号，这种划分源自遗留的特征工程实践，与底层决策过程不一致。用户行为本质上是同质上下文单元的序列，历史和当前请求仅在结果是否观测上有所不同，将它们视为异构信号会掩盖用户决策上下文中的结构依赖，限制了规模化效率和预测质量，尤其在电商货架和瀑布流等上下文丰富的场景中更为突出。

Method: 提出的UniCon架构以请求上下文为基本建模单元，将历史和预测目标组织为同质的上下文单元。采用上下文内注意力（Intra-context attention）建模上下文内各item之间的局部耦合（Locality），采用上下文间注意力（Inter-context attention）建模跨上下文的决策状态动态演化（Dynamics），并引入上下文单元级别的序列压缩以降低部署开销。

Result: 在美团搜索广告上，UniCon相较于强生产基线将离线AUC提升0.0139，并取得统计显著的线上收益：RPM提升3.09%，CTR提升2.07%，收入提升2.95%。

Conclusion: UniCon通过上下文中心化的统一建模方式，重新对齐了建模粒度与用户真实决策过程，弥合了历史行为与预测目标之间的结构差距，支持更有效的规模化扩展，同时兼顾了预测质量和部署效率。

Abstract: Unified modeling has become a major direction for industrial click-through rate (CTR) prediction. Existing approaches typically unify sequential and non-sequential signals at the token level, model their interactions in a shared backbone, and increase model capacity to improve scaling behavior. However, this division originates from legacy feature-engineering practice and is misaligned with the underlying decision process. User behavior is inherently a sequence of homogeneous context units; at the level of input organization, historical behavior and the current request differ only in whether their outcomes are observed or remain to be predicted. Treating them as heterogeneous signals obscures structural dependencies within the user's decision context, limiting both scaling efficiency and prediction quality. This limitation is particularly pronounced in context-rich scenarios such as e-commerce shelves and waterfall feeds. To address this, we propose UniCon, a unified context-centric modeling architecture that treats the request context as the basic modeling unit and organizes history and prediction targets as homogeneous context units. Intra-context attention captures local coupling among items within a context (Locality), while inter-context attention models the dynamic evolution of decision states across contexts (Dynamics). This organization bridges the structural gap between history and target and supports more effective scaling of unified CTR models. Context-unit-level sequence compression further reduces deployment overhead. On Meituan search advertising, UniCon improves offline AUC by 0.0139 over a strong production baseline and achieves statistically significant online lifts of 3.09% in RPM, 2.07% in CTR, and 2.95% in revenue.

</details>


### [113] [DoPR: Reusable Compressed Document Prefixes for Efficient LLM Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03311)
*Beiya Dai,Yifan Wei,Guang Yang,Xing Shi,Xinbing Wang,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DoPR框架，通过离线预计算文档的压缩前缀状态并在在线重排序时复用，实现重排序任务的文档端计算解耦与加速，在TREC DL、BEIR和BRIGHT基准上达到最高8.04倍延迟加速，同时保留97.1%-99.5%的重排性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为重排序器时，逐点重排序会在不同查询间反复处理同一文档，导致大量冗余的文档端计算，造成巨大的在线推理开销。

Method: DoPR将文档处理与在线重排序解耦：首先选择查询无关的文档表示并转换为压缩的文档前缀状态，该状态在离线阶段预计算并可在文档被检索时复用；在线重排序时，仅需处理查询和评分标记，文档信息通过存储的前缀状态提供，实现文档端压缩与前缀状态跨查询复用双重降本。

Result: 在TREC DL、BEIR和BRIGHT基准上，使用Qwen3模型（0.6B至8B）验证，DoPR实现最高8.0倍文档端内存缩减和8.04倍延迟加速，同时保留97.1%-99.5%的NDCG@10性能。

Conclusion: DoPR通过将文档处理转为离线可复用的压缩前缀状态，有效消除了点式重排序中的冗余文档端计算，在保持接近完整文档重排序器性能的前提下实现了显著的在线效率提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are effective rerankers, but pointwise reranking repeatedly processes the same document across different queries, causing substantial redundant document-side computation. We propose \textbf{DoPR}, a compressed document prefix framework that decouples offline document processing from online reranking. DoPR first selects query-independent document representations and converts them into compressed document prefix states, which are precomputed offline and reused whenever the document is retrieved. During online reranking, the model scores each query-document pair by processing only the query and scoring token, with document information supplied by the stored prefix states. This design reduces online cost through both document-side compression and cross-query prefix-state reuse. Experiments on TREC DL, BEIR, and BRIGHT with Qwen3 models from $0.6$B to $8$B show that DoPR achieves up to 8.0$\times$ online document-side memory reduction and up to 8.04$\times$ latency speedup, while retaining \textbf{97.1\%-99.5\%} of the average NDCG@10 of matched full-document rerankers.

</details>


### [114] [SelfDR: Self-Distillation from Reasoning for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03313)
*Chumeng Jiang,Jiayin Wang,Xinjie Lin,Zhiqiang Guo,Hengliang Luo,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SelfDR提出了一种基于自我蒸馏的框架，将LLM的推理增强预测蒸馏为直接推荐结果，在提升推荐效果的同时保持推理效率，且所有组件基于同一基础LLM构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在推荐系统中展现出强大能力，但显式生成中间推理过程会带来高昂的计算成本，限制了其在实际推荐系统中的部署。

Method: 提出SelfDR框架，包含教师推荐器（训练reasoner并以下游性能为奖励，生成针对性推理并纳入输入）和学生推荐器（通过动态权重的自我蒸馏直接从教师学习，无需外部模型）。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验验证了SelfDR的有效性、合理性和效率。

Conclusion: SelfDR通过自我蒸馏策略，在不依赖外部模型的情况下，成功实现了推理增强的推荐效果与高效推理之间的平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful backbones for recommendation. To better elicit their capabilities, reasoning has been widely incorporated to help LLMs interpret rich textual signals and improve recommendation accuracy. However, explicitly generating intermediate reasoning traces often incurs substantial computational costs, which limits practical deployment in real-world recommender systems. To address this challenge, we propose SelfDR, a Self-Distillation from Reasoning framework for LLM-based Recommendation. SelfDR distills an LLM's own reasoning-enhanced predictions to produce recommendations directly, improving recommendation effectiveness while maintaining inference efficiency. All components in the framework are built on the same base LLM, without relying on any external models. Specifically, the teacher recommender is constructed by training a reasoner with downstream performance as the reward, enabling it to generate targeted rationales that are later incorporated into the teacher's input. A student recommender for direct recommendation, with the same underlying model, then learns from the teacher through self-distillation with a dynamic weighting strategy. Extensive experiments on three public datasets validate the effectiveness, rationality, and efficiency of SelfDR. Codes are available at https://github.com/JiangDeccc/SelfDistillation.

</details>


### [115] [SciLENS: RL-Driven Autonomous Agents for Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03338)
*Leqi Zheng,Jinbo Su,Yuying Li,Chaokun Wang,Weiping Wang,Haitao Li,Jiajun Zhang,Shannan Yan,Zhaolu Kang,Rong Fu,Jie Wu,Fang Niu,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciLENS，一个完全本地化的自主科学文献综合代理框架，基于约1200万条学术记录的双层基础设施构建。该框架将结构化可视化作推理循环中的可操作工具，通过自动数据合成管道（基于20个前沿模型的跨模型共识验证）训练代理，并采用逆向分解评估策略进行对齐。在六个科学基准上的评估显示其显著优于开源基线，性能可与GPT-5.2和Gemini-3.0-pro相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学文献综合代理过度依赖专有在线服务，导致可复现性差、隐私泄露风险高、无法离线部署。为解决这些问题，研究者希望构建一个完全本地的自主代理框架。

Method: (1) 构建双层基础设施，索引约1200万条学术记录；(2) 将结构可视化集成到推理循环中作为可操作工具，将复杂引文拓扑压缩为经过验证的数据驱动图表，缓解宏观综合期间的上下文耗尽问题；(3) 开发无需人工标注的自动数据合成管道，从引文知识图谱中提取多跳子图，经20个前沿模型的跨模型共识验证；(4) 通过逆向分解评估策略实现对齐，为早期规划和严格证据接地提供细粒度过程奖励。

Result: 在涵盖标准问答、引文准确性、事实推理和结构综合的六个科学基准上，SciLENS显著优于开源基线，性能与GPT-5.2和Gemini-3.0-pro相当。

Conclusion: SciLENS证明了完全本地化的科学文献综合框架的可行性，在可复现性、隐私和离线部署方面具有优势，同时达到与顶尖商业模型相当的性能水平。代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Scientific literature synthesis agents increasingly rely on proprietary online services, limiting reproducibility, privacy, and offline deployment. To address this challenge, we introduce SciLENS Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis), a fully local autonomous agent framework operating on a dual-tier infrastructure indexing approximately 12 million academic records. SciLENS pioneers the integration of structural visualization as an actionable tool within the reasoning loop, enabling the agent to compress complex citation topologies into validated data-driven charts and thereby mitigate context exhaustion during macro-level synthesis. To train the agent without human annotation, we develop an automated data synthesis pipeline that extracts multi-hop subgraphs from a citation knowledge graph, verified by cross-model consensus among 20 frontier models. The agent is subsequently aligned through a reverse-decomposition rubric strategy that provides fine-grained process rewards for early planning and strict evidence grounding. Evaluations across six scientific benchmarks encompassing standard QA, citation accuracy, factual reasoning, and structural synthesis demonstrate that SciLENS significantly outperforms open-source baselines and achieves performance comparable to GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3.0-pro. Our source code and data are released at https://github.com/LQgdwind/SciLENS.

</details>


### [116] [HypRQ-VAE: Hyperbolic Item Indexing for Long-Tail-Aware Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03369)
*Longfeng Wu,Tong Zeng,Giovanni Seni,Zhimin Peng,Bhanu Pratap Singh Rawat,Si Zhang,Yao Zhou,Lecheng Zheng,Bo Ji,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HypRQ-VAE，首个在双曲空间中学习物品索引的框架，用于解决生成式推荐中LLM文本令牌与离散物品索引之间的不匹配问题。该方法利用双曲几何的指数体积扩展特性来建模真实目录的长尾分布，在三个基准数据集上显著提升了推荐性能，尤其是在尾部物品推荐方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法将推荐作为语言建模任务，但LLM基于文本令牌而推荐系统依赖离散物品索引，这种错位导致幻觉问题。此外，现有方法在欧几里得空间中学习物品词汇，难以建模真实目录中头物品主导、大量尾部物品反映用户小众偏好的固有长尾分布。

Method: 提出双曲残差量化变分自编码器（HypRQ-VAE），利用双曲几何的指数体积扩展特性来自然地适应用户-物品交互的幂律结构，在双曲空间中学习物品索引，既能编码丰富的文本语义，又能保持稀疏长尾物品的表征保真度。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，HypRQ-VAE显著提升了推荐性能，尤其在推荐尾部物品方面表现突出。分析归因于双曲空间在建模物品层级结构和稀疏性方面的优越能力。

Conclusion: 双曲空间为生成式推荐中的物品索引问题提供了更优的解决方案，其几何特性天然契合物品目录的长尾分布结构，能够有效缓解幻觉问题并提升长尾推荐能力。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems model user behavior as item ID sequences, while recent generative methods cast recommendation as a language modeling task using large language models (LLMs). While this paradigm incorporates rich textual semantics, it introduces a fundamental mismatch: LLMs operate on text tokens, whereas recommender systems depend on discrete item indices. This misalignment often leads to hallucinations in generative recommendations. Existing methods attempt to bridge this gap by learning item vocabularies in Euclidean space, but they struggle to model the inherent long-tail distribution of real-world catalogs, where a small number of head items dominate, and a vast number of tail items reflect users' niche preferences. To address this issue, we introduce Hyperbolic Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder (HypRQ-VAE), the first framework to learn item indexing in hyperbolic space. HypRQ-VAE leverages the unique properties of hyperbolic geometry, whose exponential volume expansion naturally accommodates the power law structure of user-item interactions. This allows the model to encode rich textual semantics while preserving the representational fidelity of sparse, long-tail items. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that HypRQ-VAE significantly improves the performance of recommendation, particularly in recommending tail items. Our analysis attributes these gains to the superior capacity of hyperbolic space to model item hierarchies and sparsity in generative recommendation. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/wulongfeng/HypRQ-VAE.

</details>


### [117] [Plan Pointers and Record-Directive Form in Budgeted Verification of Inherited Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03450)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过12项注册研究（14,760次尝试）测量了六条记忆智能体在不同指令形式下对存档记录的检索行为。研究发现：长度匹配的标准在直接供应商模型上比裸ID高出+35.0分，但在OpenRouter九模型面板上未通过优越性规则；追加ID在三款Claude模型上完全取消了标准的效果（Opus 5从40/40降至0/40），但在第二存储库中所有模型均遵循标准；批准线(+96.0)和两个信用点预算恢复了三款模型上目标检索。所有结果均描述为固定面板上的精确编辑效果，不做机制声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究继承六条单行记忆的智能体在行动前最多检索一条存档记录时，存储中写入的指令（指针、标准或二者兼有）如何引导其选择，以理解不同指令形式对模型行为的影响差异。

Method: 采用注册研究报告框架，在同一仪器谱系的12项研究中开展14,760次尝试。设计了多种指令条件：裸ID、长度匹配标准、ID+标准组合、批准线、信用预算、单字符计划指针等，对Claude系列（Opus 5等）、Fable 5.1及直接提供者和OpenRouter多模型面板进行对比测试，并进行了前瞻性注册的重复运行（B'和G'）与字节匹配编辑（Study G）等控制实验。

Result: 长度匹配标准在六个直接提供者模型上比裸ID高+35.0点[+31.2,+38.8]，但在OpenRouter九模型面板上未通过注册优越性规则。追加ID完全抵消了标准效果：三款Claude模型上标准从40/40降至0/40（Opus 5）。批准线(+96.0点)和两信用预算恢复目标。在第二存储库中所有模型遵循标准。标准使决策在Opus 5上向当前记录移动+100.0点，但在Fable 5.1上完全背离。单字符计划指针效果+78.0点，注册重跑+81.7点。三十个复制对比中十五个在边际内，十五个未解决。

Conclusion: 指令形式对智能体存档检索行为有显著且情境依赖的影响：标准在直接提供者模型上有效但在多模型面板上失败；后缀取消效果在Claude模型上高度鲁棒但在第二存储库中消失。所有发现作为固定面板上精确编辑的描述性效果报告，不主张普遍机制结论，强调需要更深入理解不同模型和存储环境下的指令交互机制。

Abstract: An agent that inherits six one-line memories may pull at most one archived source record before acting; a directive written into the store can steer that choice: a pointer to the record, a criterion that identifies it, or both. Across twelve registered studies on one instrument lineage (14,760 attempts) we measured where the request goes under each form. On six direct-provider models a length-matched criterion exceeded a bare id by +35.0 points [+31.2, +38.8] (Study D); the contrast failed its registered superiority rule on a nine-model OpenRouter-served panel (Study E). Appending the id cancelled the criterion on three Claude models (Opus 5: 40/40 to 0/40; Study F-x); six byte-matched edits gave each exact string its own effect (Study G), and a re-run at eighty runs per cell left fifteen of thirty replication contrasts within the margin, fifteen unresolved and none beyond (Study G'). A ratification line (+96.0 points on Opus 5) and a budget of two credits restored the target on all three (Study J); across five criterion strings the suffix's cancellation held for four of the five wordings on Opus 5 and all five wordings on Fable 5.1 (Study H2); in a second store every model followed the criterion (Study H1). Continued into a decision, the criterion moved the choice toward the current record (+100.0 points, Opus 5) and away from it on Fable 5.1 (Study I). A one-character plan pointer's effect (+78.0 points; Study B, after a correction of its first repository report) returned the same verdict under a prospectively registered re-run (+81.7 points; Study B'). All results are descriptive effects of exact edits on fixed panels with registered intervals and no mechanism claim.

</details>


### [118] [ExplainRoute: A Pre-Deployment Audit Framework for Non-Answer-Giving Programming Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03470)
*Yiming Gai,Yingying Zhang,Xuefei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ExplainRoute，一个用于非直接给答案的编程辅导系统的部署前审计框架。它估计学习者的解释状态并选择两种有界响应之一（费曼式自我解释提示或苏格拉底式支架），通过机器可检查的合约暴露其状态、策略、引用代码片段和泄漏风险。离线评估使用1,770对SelfCode语料库，结果表明契约有效性达100%，但自适应路由并未优于固定策略，最终贡献是验证了审计协议和边界发现，而非学习效果的改进证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程辅导系统应支持学习者自身的解释而非直接提供模型答案，但现有基准仅根据流畅度对辅导系统进行排名，缺乏对信息边界、响应极性、失败闭合性和学习者解释可见性价值的系统性审计方法。

Method: 提出ExplainRoute预部署审计框架：(1) 输入代码行和学习者解释，估计解释状态并选择两种有界响应（费曼式自我解释提示或苏格拉底式支架）；(2) 通过机器可检查的合约暴露状态、策略、引用代码片段和泄漏风险；(3) 在1,770对SelfCode语料库上采用代码组划分进行离线评估，其中443对保留在11个未触碰的保持组中；(4) 比较直接回答、固定开放式自我解释、固定苏格拉底式支架、自适应路由和无状态消融等多种条件。

Result: 契约有效性在所有教学条件下达到100%。自适应路由在60.5%的记录上与冻结参考规则匹配，状态宏F1为0.238（开放：0.229；苏格拉底：0.246），显示无可靠的自适应优势。独立语言模型裁判评分为自适应响应4.516/5，优于无状态消融（2.819/5），但略低于固定开放式自我解释（4.598/5）和苏格拉底支架（4.658/5）。在分层的40行子集上的盲评评估确认可见的学习者解释能提高信息价值，但自适应路由并未优于固定策略。

Conclusion: ExplainRoute的贡献是一个经过验证的审计协议和基础性边界发现，而非改进学习、保留率或因果教学有效性的证据。这表明在部署前审计辅导系统的信息边界和响应策略是有价值的，但当前自适应路由并未展现出超越固定策略的可靠优势。

Abstract: Programming tutors should support learners' own explanations rather than immediately providing model answers. We present ExplainRoute, a pre-deployment audit framework for non-answer-giving programming tutors. Given a code line and a learner explanation, it estimates the explanation state and selects one of two bounded responses: a Feynman-style self-explanation prompt or a Socratic scaffold. The framework exposes its state, strategy, cited code fragment, and leakage risk through a machine-checkable contract. Unlike benchmarks that rank tutors by fluency alone, ExplainRoute audits information boundaries, response polarity, failure closure, and the value of learner-explanation visibility before classroom deployment. We evaluate it offline on the 1,770-pair SelfCode corpus using a code-group split, with 443 pairs reserved in 11 untouched holdout groups. The evaluation compares direct answers, fixed open self-explanation, fixed Socratic scaffolding, adaptive routing, and an adaptive no-state ablation. Contract validity reaches 100% for all pedagogical conditions. Adaptive routing matches the frozen reference rule on 60.5% of records, with state macro-F1 of 0.238 (Open: 0.229; Socratic: 0.246), showing no reliable adaptive advantage. An independent language-model judge scores adaptive responses 4.516/5, outperforming the no-state ablation (2.819/5) but slightly below fixed open self-explanation (4.598/5) and Socratic scaffolding (4.658/5). A blinded rubric evaluation on a stratified 40-row subset confirms that visible learner explanations improve information value while adaptive routing does not outperform fixed strategies. The contribution is a validated audit protocol and a boundary finding, rather than evidence of improved learning, retention, or causal instructional effectiveness.

</details>


### [119] [From Topical Relevance to Answerability: Entailment Distillation for Conversational Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03482)
*Shuai Qin,Guojia An,Weikang Guo,Pei Ke,Jiwei Wei,Yang Yang,Jie Zou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR框架，解决对话检索中将主题相关性等同于可回答性的系统性偏差（答案鸿沟）。核心是通过蕴含蒸馏将答案-段落蕴含监督转移到跨编码器重排序器，使其在推理时无需答案即可区分支持答案的段落与主题干扰项；同时辅以段落中心的反事实召回模块，利用LLM从段落推断可回答查询以扩充候选池。在TopiOCQA、QReCC和域外TREC CAsT数据集上均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话检索器将主题相关性作为可回答性的代理指标，但紧密匹配对话上下文的段落未必支持正确答案，导致系统性可回答性鸿沟（answerability gap）。

Method: 1) 蕴含蒸馏（entailment distillation）：将答案-段落蕴含监督转移到跨编码器重排序器，推理时无需真实答案即可辨别支持答案的段落。2) 段落中心的反事实召回（passage-centric abductive recall）：利用LLM从段落反推可回答查询，将低相似度但可回答的段落纳入候选池。

Result: 在TopiOCQA、QReCC及域外TREC CAsT数据集上，CLEAR一致提升top-ranked精度，优于查询改写和稠密检索强基线；在对话含较重主题噪声时提升最大。将重排序器叠加在LLM驱动的查询改写器之上可获进一步增益。

Conclusion: CLEAR证明将对话检索从主题相关性转向可回答性是有效路径，尤其面对主题噪声场景时优势显著，且可模块化地与其他检索组件（如LLM查询改写器）叠加使用。

Abstract: Existing conversational retrievers commonly treat topical relevance as a proxy for answerability. However, a passage that closely matches the dialogue context is not necessarily the one that supports the correct answer. We identify this mismatch as a systematic answerability gap. To address this issue, we propose CLEAR, a framework that shifts conversational retrieval from topical relevance to answerability. The core of CLEAR is entailment distillation, which transfers answer-passage entailment supervision into a cross-encoder reranker so that the reranker discriminates answer-supporting passages from topical distractors at inference time, without requiring answers. CLEAR is complemented by a passage-centric abductive recall module that brings low-similarity yet answerable passages into the candidate pool by inferring answerable queries from passages with an LLM. Across TopiOCQA, QReCC, and out-of-domain TREC CAsT datasets, CLEAR consistently improves top-ranked precision over strong query-rewriting and dense-retrieval baselines, with the largest gains observed in conversations involving heavier topical noise. Moreover, applying our reranker on top of an LLM-driven query rewriter yields further gains.

</details>


### [120] [EPIC: Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning for Semantic ID Diffusion Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03522)
*Tuan-Binh Tran,Thanh Tam Nguyen,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Dung D. Le,Tung Kieu,Thanh Trung Huynh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID) generative recommendation predicts the next item by generating a short tuple of discrete tokens. Recent masked-diffusion methods improve this process through bidirectional context and flexible decoding, yet recommendation ultimately requires selecting among complete catalog items. At each denoising step, a partial SID can correspond to multiple feasible items, while existing methods primarily reason through position-wise token predictions. We propose Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning (EPIC), which introduces explicit item-level competition into SID denoising. EPIC constructs a personalized posterior over feasible candidate items using the current generation context and the user's recent interactions, then projects this distribution back to unresolved SID positions to guide subsequent token decisions. The pretrained backbone remains frozen and requires no additional decoder forward pass. Experiments on four Amazon benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong baselines, while diagnostic analyses indicate that the gains primarily arise from personalized transition evidence that preserves promising item hypotheses during denoising.

</details>


### [121] [LLM4AIGQ: LLM-based AI Guidance Query Generation Framework for Multi Interest Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03674)
*Xiangchen Pan,Jiayi Xu,Jing Wang,Xing Fang,Lingyun Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM4AIGQ，一个基于大语言模型的AI引导查询生成方案，通过整合用户画像和历史交互序列来细分用户兴趣、推断各子兴趣下的消费意图并生成对应的引导查询，采用SFT、RL和DPO的后训练流程及多级奖励设计，配合近线生成与在线读取的部署架构，在离线评测和在线A/B测试中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI生成查询依赖"查询到AI生成查询"的两阶段关联范式，存在信息级联丢失导致的语义漂移问题；且主要查询的推导过度依赖"用户-商品"共现关系，缺乏对用户多兴趣的探索，导致引导查询价值低、与购买意图不匹配。

Method: 提出LLM4AIGQ方案：首先整合用户画像和交互序列细分用户兴趣，推断每个子兴趣对应的具体消费意图，进而生成相应的AIGQ；训练方面采用包含监督微调(SFT)、强化学习(RL)和直接偏好优化(DPO)的后训练流水线，并设计多级奖励以支持多目标优化和长链推理；部署上采用近线生成与在线读取的架构以满足延迟约束。

Result: 广泛实验表明，该模型在离线评测和在线A/B测试中均取得了稳健的性能表现。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的兴趣细分与多级奖励训练方案能够有效克服传统共现检索的表达局限，生成更贴合用户多兴趣与购买意图的高价值引导查询，其近线-在线结合的部署架构也能满足实际应用延迟要求。

Abstract: Guidance queries stimulate user consumption by extracting preferences to provide search queries with guidance value, playing a crucial role in the e-commerce field. Traditional AI-generated queries (AIGQ) generation primarily relies on a two-stage "Query-to-AI-Generated-Query" (Q2AIGQ) association paradigm, first recalling user primary search queries from user profiles, historical behavior sequences, item-side information, and the current query through multi-path retrieval, then generalizing AIGQ via rule-based methods. This approach suffers from semantic drift due to information cascade loss; additionally, primary search query derivation heavily depends on "user-item" co-occurrence relationships, lacking exploration of user multi-interests, resulting in guidance queries with low value and mismatched purchase intent. To address the expressive limitations of traditional co-occurrence-based retrieval, we propose LLM4AIGQ, an LLM-based solution for generating AI guidance queries tailored to users' multi-interests. This approach segments user interests by integrating user profiles and historical interaction sequences, infers specific consumption intents for each sub-interest, and subsequently generates corresponding AIGQ. In terms of model training, we employ a post-training pipeline comprising Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Reinforcement Learning (RL), and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to enhance the model's capability in generating AIGQ. We also introduce a multi-level reward design to satisfy the requirements of multi-objective optimization and long-chain reasoning in practical applications. Regarding deployment, we adopt a nearline-generation and online-read architecture to meet latency constraints. Extensive experimental analyses demonstrate that our model achieves robust performance in both offline evaluations and online A/B tests.

</details>


### [122] [Unified Pitch Graphs for Diagnosing Pitching Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03810)
*Kichang Lee,JeongGil Ko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出统一投球图(UPG)，一种层次化图表示方法，用于回溯性分析棒球投球序列中的时空事件。UPG保留每次投球的精确三维轨迹和上下文，通过有向序列边连接连续投球，并支持自适应回退机制，在缺乏重复证据时从精细长序列回退，同时保留事件谱系。在394万次MLB Statcast投球数据上，该方法将持有路径覆盖率从18.9%提升至94.9%，执行重构R²从0.495提升至0.685。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有棒球投球策略的表征方式将投球压缩为离散类型或聚合统计，丢失了物理执行细节和投球使用的有序上下文信息，无法捕捉同一类型投球在不同序列语境中的细微差异及策略模式。

Method: 提出Unified Pitch Graphs(UPG)层次化图表示：(1)将每次投球保留为带有重建三维轨迹和上下文的精确事件；(2)用有向序列边连接连续投球；(3)跨语义和时间分辨率组织事件；(4)采用支持自适应机制，当重复证据不足时从精细长序列退避，同时保留精确事件谱系。

Result: 在2021-2026年394万次MLB Statcast投球上验证：名义上相同的投球序列表现出不同的物理执行；支持自适应回退将持有路径覆盖率从18.9%提升至94.9%，执行重构R²从0.495提升至0.685；UPG能可靠定位离散投球组合和序列表示无法检测的受控执行变化。

Conclusion: UPG提供了一种可追溯、多尺度的层次化表示方法，能够识别重复出现的策略模式，同时避免将回溯性关联混淆为因果或未来表现的主张。

Abstract: Pitching strategy in baseball is expressed through both physical execution and the ordered context in which pitches are used, yet common representations collapse pitches into discrete types or aggregate statistics. We present Unified Pitch Graphs (UPG), a hierarchical graph representation for retrospective analysis of sequential spatiotemporal events. UPG preserves each pitch as an exact event with reconstructed three-dimensional trajectory and context, connects consecutive pitches through directed sequence edges, and organizes the same events across semantic and temporal resolutions. A support-adaptive mechanism backs off from fine, long sequences when repeated evidence is insufficient, while retaining exact event lineage. We evaluate UPG on 3.94 million MLB Statcast pitches from 2021 to 2026. Nominally identical pitch sequences exhibit distinct physical executions, and ordered structure becomes increasingly evident in longer context-conditioned paths. Support-adaptive backoff increases held-out path coverage from 18.9\% to 94.9\% while improving execution reconstruction from $R^2=0.495$ to $0.685$. UPG also reliably localizes controlled execution changes that discrete pitch-mix and sequence representations cannot detect. These results demonstrate that UPG provides a traceable, multi-scale representation for identifying recurring strategy patterns without conflating retrospective associations with causal or future-performance claims.

</details>


### [123] [GRASP: Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline for Label-Free Multi-Topic Essay Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03857)
*Aafreen Husain,Samar Shailendra,Saad Sajid Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为GRASP的图检索自动评分流水线，用于对无标签的多主题科学考试短文进行自动评分。该方法通过Sentence-BERT编码参考答案并构建语义相似度图，利用句子计数启发式和大语言模型预测学生作文包含的主题数量，再结合RAG和GRAG两种检索方式获取参考答案候选节点，最后通过匈牙利算法实现最优分配并对每个段落独立评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动短文评分研究主要聚焦于单主题考试，而对包含多个主题的考试自动评分探索不足。尤其当学生的多主题回答被合并为单一无标记段落时，如何准确识别并评分每个主题对应的答案片段是一个尚未充分解决的难题。

Method: GRASP流水线包含以下步骤：(1)使用Sentence-BERT将各问题的参考答案编码到FAISS向量索引中，并构建语义相似度图；(2)利用句子计数启发式结合大语言模型预测学生作文中的主题数量；(3)通过余弦相似度RAG和图检索GRAG获取候选参考节点（GRAG从top余弦匹配作为种子节点出发，沿强边图遍历寻找更多参考）；(4)使用匈牙利算法实现参考节点与问题段落的最优分配；(5)使用GPT-4.1-mini对各段落独立评分。

Result: 实验旨在展示检索质量对评分准确度的影响，以及在不同作文复杂度水平下图增强检索相对纯余弦相似度方法的优势。

Conclusion: 该研究无需训练数据或特定领域的示例作文即可实现标签自由场景下的多主题短文自动评分，验证了图检索增强在提升检索覆盖率和评分准确性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Automated short-answer grading research has historically focused on exams consisting solely of questions pertaining to a single topic. Automatic grading of exams containing questions about more than one topic remains less explored. In this work, a Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline (GRASP) is introduced for grading label-free multi-topic science exams. Label-free exams are short-answer exams in which a student's responses to several distinct topics are merged into a single paragraph, with no markup labels or segmentation indicating which span answers which question. Reference answers for each question are encoded into a FAISS vector index via Sentence-BERT, and a semantic similarity graph is constructed over this set of reference answers. At grading time, sentence count heuristics, with a large language model used to resolve ambiguous cases, are first applied to predict how many distinct topics were answered in the student essay. This process is performed without training data or domain-specific example essays. Candidate reference nodes, each storing one (question, reference answer, concatenation of both) from the reference index, are then retrieved through cosine similarity based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GRAG). GRAG operates by taking the top cosine matches as seed nodes and then performing a graph traversal over strong edges to find additional reference nodes that may have been missed by RAG. The Hungarian algorithm is then used to optimally assign one reference node per question segment such that no reference is duplicated. Each segment is then graded against its assigned reference independently using GPT-4.1-mini. This experiment is performed to show the effect of retrieval quality on grading accuracy and the benefit of graph-augmented retrieval versus strict cosine similarity methods at various levels of essay complexity.

</details>


### [124] [Comparing Retrieval Methods for Academic Advisor Discovery: A Six-Method Study of 768 CS Faculty Profiles Across 9 US Universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03901)
*Biraj Subedi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对六种信息检索方法在"学术导师发现"任务上进行了比较评估，使用768份教师档案和162条相关性判断构建的新领域数据集。结果显示Reranked方法在NDCG@10上表现最佳（0.477），场消融实验发现仅使用个人简介（0.634）优于组合模型（0.593），且拼接arXiv论文摘要会降低性能0.176，支持晚期融合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术导师发现是研究生申请过程中的关键任务，但缺乏针对该任务的系统性方法比较和领域专属评估数据集，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了包含768份来自9个美国CS系的教师档案和5个查询（162条0/1/2三级相关性判断）的数据集，比较六种方法：Jaccard重叠、TF-IDF、BM25（稀疏词法匹配）、all-MiniLM-L6-v2句嵌入（稠密语义检索）、混合分数融合以及学习排序（Reranked）。同时进行场消融和arXiv摘要拼接受控实验。

Result: Reranked获得最高平均NDCG@10（0.477，std 0.138），其次为Semantic（0.450）、Hybrid（0.421）、BM25（0.406）、Jaccard（0.303）、TF-IDF（0.246）。Bonferroni校正后仅TF-IDF显著差于BM25、Semantic、Hybrid和Reranked。场消融显示仅用个人简介（0.634）优于完整模型（0.593）。拼接arXiv摘要使NDCG降低0.176。

Conclusion: 学习排序方法在学术导师发现任务中表现最优；个人简介是比领域标签更有效的特征来源；直接拼接论文摘要文本会损害检索性能，应优先采用晚期融合架构。所有代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: We present a comparative evaluation of six information retrieval methods for the task of academic advisor discovery: ranking CS faculty members by relevance to a graduate applicant's research interest statement. The methods span sparse lexical matching (Jaccard overlap, TF-IDF, BM25), dense semantic retrieval (all-MiniLM-L6-v2 sentence embeddings), hybrid score fusion, and learning-to-rank. Evaluation uses a new domain-specific collection: 768 faculty profiles scraped from 9 US CS departments, with 162 graded relevance judgments (grade 0/1/2) across 5 queries representing distinct graduate student research profiles. Across all five queries, Reranked achieves the highest mean NDCG@10 (0.477, std 0.138), followed by Semantic (0.450), Hybrid (0.421), BM25 (0.406), Jaccard (0.303), and TF-IDF (0.246). After Bonferroni correction across all 15 pairwise comparisons, TF-IDF is significantly worse than BM25, Semantic, Hybrid, and Reranked; no other pairwise difference survives correction at 5 queries. A field ablation reveals that biography alone (NDCG 0.634) outperforms the full model combining biography with research area tags (0.593). A controlled experiment shows that concatenating arXiv paper abstracts reduces NDCG@10 by 0.176, motivating a late-fusion architecture. All code, scrapers, and relevance labels are released openly.

</details>


### [125] [The WebKurator.de Platform: Combined Regional and Topical Web Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03971)
*Michael Dinzinger,Natanael Arndt,Ben Böck,Jelena Mitrović,Michael Granitzer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出WebKurator.de平台，一种结合区域与主题的协作式德国网络策展系统，采用二维策展模型分离主题分类与地理标注，基于554万网站数据集引导启动，其中258万德国网站完成地址提取和主题标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家图书馆和记忆机构在网络策展中面临挑战，现有基于目录的方法（如Curlie）采用主题中心的一维层级结构，地理因素与主题、语言类别交织在一起，缺乏对区域相关内容的有效策展。

Method: 提出两维策展模型（二维度：主题分类+地理标注），集成基于LLM的主题分类和基于印鉴页的地址提取与地理编码技术，支持用户建议与人工审核机制，并使用德国印鉴数据集（554万网站）进行引导启动。

Result: 从554万网站中识别出314万含有印鉴页的网站，成功提取并地理编码其中地址；其中258万（85.17%）位于德国并获得主题标签，构成平台的初始基础，可持续通过用户建议扩展。

Conclusion: WebKurator.de通过创新的二维策展模型突破了传统一维目录体系的局限，实现了地理与主题维度的有机分离与结合，为面向特定国家/地区的精细化网络策展提供了可行的协作平台方案。

Abstract: The systematic curation of the Web remains a central challenge for national libraries and memory institutions that aim to preserve culturally and regionally relevant content. Existing directory-based approaches such as Curlie implement a predominantly topic-centric, one-dimensional hierarchy, where geographic aspects are intertwined with topical and linguistic categories. To address this limitation, we present WebKurator.de, a collaborative platform for combined regional and topical web curation, initially focused on the German web. WebKurator introduces a two-dimensional curation model that explicitly separates topical categorization and geographic annotation. The system integrates LLM-based topic classification and imprint-based address extraction with geocoding, and supports user suggestions together with moderated review. The platform is bootstrapped from the German Imprints Dataset, a large-scale collection of 5.54 million websites. Among them, 3.14 million contain imprint pages, for which we successfully extracted and geocoded postal addresses. Of these, 2.58 million (85.17%) are located in Germany and also have an assigned topic label. These websites form the initial foundation of WebKurator.de and can be continuously extended through user suggestions.

</details>


### [126] [The Dice Roll Method: A Standardized Protocol for Repeated-Query Auditing of Large Language Model Brand Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04047)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了"骰子滚动法"(Dice Roll Method)，作为重复提示审计大语言模型品牌推荐的标准化协议，通过方差分解、负二项混合模型、Cliff's delta等统计工具，将迭代次数分为探索性(n=5)、验证性(n=10)和严格性(n=15)三档，并在39个外部验证单元中复现了37个可靠性预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越频繁地使用重复相同提示来审计大语言模型品牌推荐中的随机变异，但缺乏标准化的协议来设定迭代次数、选择稳定性指标或建立可靠性阈值。

Method: 将总响应方差分解为采样、提示措辞、运行间和模型版本四个成分；核心方法包括：以迭代为重复测量的负二项混合模型、分布无关效应量Cliff's delta、保留依赖性的bootstrap、基于仿真的功效分析、概化理论分解以及固定快照上的漂移诊断。重新分析了五个品牌推荐审计研究，约19万观测值、270多个品牌、6种语言、迭代数5至40。

Result: D-study得出三档迭代指南：探索性(n=5, G=0.58)、验证性(n=10, G=0.74)、严格性(n=15, G=0.81)。四大度量族（计数、集合、嵌入、公平调整PASOR）具有互补性，支持紧凑度量组而非单一指标。预注册外部验证（三个独立语料库共39个单元）中37个复现成功，无失败案例；n=5的功效值精确到两位小数吻合，但固定档位不具迁移性，支持先试点后求解的解读。

Conclusion: 该协议为重复查询审计大语言模型品牌推荐提供了统计原理基础，能够应对真实自回归生成中的条件性和非高斯结构。

Abstract: Background: Researchers increasingly use repeated identical prompts to audit stochastic variation in large language model (LLM) brand recommendations, yet no standardized protocol exists for setting iteration counts, selecting stability metrics, or establishing reliability thresholds. Objective: We formalize the Dice Roll Method as a reusable protocol for repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations, grounded in a generative model of temperature-scaled nucleus sampling. Methods: Total response variance is decomposed into sampling, prompt-phrasing, run-to-run, and model-version components. The stack: a negative-binomial mixed model with iterations as repeated measures; Cliff's delta as the distribution-free effect size; dependence-preserving bootstrap; simulation-based power; a generalizability-theory decomposition; drift diagnostics on pinned snapshots. We reanalyse five brand-recommendation auditing studies: approximately 190,000 observations, 270+ brands, 6 languages, iteration counts 5 to 40. Results: Three tiers of iteration guidance emerge from the D-study: exploratory (n = 5, G = 0.58), confirmatory (n = 10, G = 0.74), and rigorous (n = 15, G = 0.81), tied to effect-size and generalizability targets. The four metric families (count, set, embedding, fairness-adjusted PASOR) are complementary, motivating a compact metric battery over single indicators. A pre-registered external validation on three independent corpora (Motoki et al., 100-round; Rozado, 24 models; llm-stability) reproduces the D-study reliability prediction in 37 of 39 cells with no failures and the n = 5 power value to two decimals; the fixed tiers do not transfer, supporting a pilot-then-solve reading. Conclusion: The protocol gives repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations a statistically principled footing under the conditional, non-Gaussian structure of real autoregressive generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [The Illusion of Independent Quorums: Epistemic Fault Domains and Correlated Cognitive Failures in Agentic Quorums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02925)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知故障域"(EFDs)和结构化认知割κ_E的概念，用于量化授权联盟在面对上游共享输入失效时的真实弹性冗余度，并证明大型仲裁团可能仅具有κ_E=1的脆弱性，最后设计了DAQC控制器在运行时强制执行结构化割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体仲裁系统常被用于高风险的基建与策略变更授权，但不同审核者常共享上游遥测、文档或工具后端。当上游输入失败时，多个投票会坍缩到同一个损坏根源上——复制并不意味着认知上的冗余。这一认知冗余假象构成了安全隐患。

Method: 本文提出Epistemic Fault Domains (EFDs)概念，定义了Structured Epistemic Cut κ_E（相对显式认知故障基，覆盖授权联盟所需的最少建模根故障数量），并在闭合因果核算、保守暴露和授权对齐条件下证明κ_E是语义妥协所需根故障数κ_S的下界。同时设计了Dependency-Aware Quorum Controller (DAQC)系统，在运行时准入阶段强制执行结构化割。

Result: 证明任意大的仲裁团可保持κ_E=1；识别共享祖先不会增加信用弹性；在兼容扩展下，固定阈值增加投票者不会增加认知割。通过解析推导和模拟验证了DAQC的机制，并提供冻结的120任务外部基准套件。

Conclusion: 复制投票者不等于认知冗余，结构化的依赖感知是保障仲裁系统真正弹性的关键。DAQC为运行时强制执行认知割提供了可行方案，且基准套件推动了该领域的可重复评估。

Abstract: Multi-agent quorums are widely used to authorize high-stakes infrastructure and policy mutations, yet distinct reviewers often share upstream telemetry, documents, or tool backends. When upstream inputs fail, multiple votes collapse onto a single corrupted cause: replication does not imply epistemic redundancy. We introduce Epistemic Fault Domains (EFDs) and the Structural Epistemic Cut κ_E, which quantifies the minimum number of modeled root faults whose exposure covers an authorizing coalition relative to an explicit Epistemic Fault Basis. Under closed causal accounting, conservative exposure, and authorization alignment, κ_E lower-bounds the number of roots required for semantic compromise (κ_S). We prove that arbitrarily large quorums can retain κ_E=1, that recognizing shared ancestry never increases credited resilience, and that adding voters at a fixed threshold cannot increase the cut under compatible exposure extensions. Finally, we design the Dependency-Aware Quorum Controller (DAQC) to enforce structural cuts at runtime admission, evaluate its mechanics via analytical derivations and simulations, and provide a frozen 120-task external benchmark suite.

</details>


### [128] [Fully Fluctuating Sleepy Consensus from Minimal Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03063)
*Javier Nieto,Yuval Efron,Joachim Neu,Ling Ren*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于睡意模型(sleepy model)的权益证明共识协议，在外部对手模型下支持完全波动的参与率，仅需PKI和VRF的极简假设即可实现诚实多数的安全性，无需VDF或硬件假设。核心创新是"分级清醒度"(graded wakeness)原语，并扩展到防腐败(uncorruption)场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币的PoW协议具有极低的参与者要求和独特的"悔改通道"（腐败矿工可以恢复诚实状态）。相比之下，基于睡意模型的PoS协议无法匹配这种鲁棒性，在普通PKI模型中必须严格限制对手参与率的波动。近期工作虽然通过外部对手模型实现了完全波动的参与率，但依赖VDF这一强密码学原语，其本质上类似于PoW的顺序计算假设。

Method: 提出"分级清醒度"(graded wakeness)原语，允许节点对其他节点是否"清醒"形成一致的见解。基于此原语设计睡意共识协议，仅需PKI和VRF即可在外部对手模型和诚实多数的假设下实现完全波动的参与率。此外，通过额外要求VRF输出的不可预测性来保证活性，协议扩展支持腐败节点的回归（uncorruption）。

Result: 成功设计了仅基于PKI和VRF的睡意共识协议，支持完全波动的参与率和外部对手模型下的诚实多数保证。消除了对VDF和硬件假设的依赖，并将协议扩展至uncorruption场景。

Conclusion: 该工作显著缩小了PoS协议与比特币PoW在鲁棒性方面的差距，证明了用极简密码学假设即可实现完全波动的参与率和腐败恢复能力，代表了睡意共识协议重要理论进展。

Abstract: Bitcoin's proof-of-work (PoW)-based protocol is remarkable for how little it asks of its participants. Not only can miners take breaks from work whenever they please, but it is almost unique in offering a path of contrition: corrupt miners can reclaim honest status simply by resuming mining on the longest chain. The protocol only requires that honest miners hold the majority of computational power at any given time. Analogous proof-of-stake (PoS) protocols, usually formalized via the sleepy model of Pass and Shi (2017), have fallen short of matching this robustness. In fact, sleepy consensus protocols in the plain PKI model must heavily restrict fluctuations in adversarial participation over time. The recent work of Efron, Neu, and Pitassi (2025) enables fully fluctuating participation in the sleepy model by introducing the external adversary model. Their protocol, however, relies on verifiable delay functions (VDFs), a strong cryptographic primitive that somewhat resembles PoW, by assuming that the adversary cannot compute sequential work significantly faster than honest nodes.
  In this work, we design a sleepy consensus protocol for fully fluctuating participation with an external adversary under an honest majority, from minimal assumptions: a public key infrastructure (PKI) and a verifiable random function (VRF). In particular, we make no VDF or hardware assumptions. Our key technique is graded wakeness, a novel primitive that allows nodes to form consistent opinions on which other nodes are awake. We further extend our protocol to handle uncorruption, where corrupt nodes return to honesty. This extension requires only a mild additional assumption on the unpredictability of VRF outputs for liveness.

</details>


### [129] [Skywing: A Platform for Decentralized Mathematical Computing in Unreliable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03145)
*Alyson Fox,Colin Ponce,Annika Mauro,Wayne Mitchell,Sarah Osborn,Tom Benson,Shayna Kapadia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Skywing，一个面向不可靠环境中去中心化数学计算的开源平台。其编程模型包含三个抽象：代理（agents）、处理器（processors）和迭代（iterations），支持异步操作与发布-订阅通信。通过共识、优化和数值线性代数等代表性算法的实验验证，展示其在不可靠网络下支持多样化去中心化算法的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算、自主系统和信息物理系统日益需要在异构设备间进行数学计算，但这些设备通过不可靠通信网络连接。传统高性能计算和分布式数据处理框架假设集中协调、可靠通信和全局同步，不适合去中心化场景，因此需要新的平台支持在不可靠环境中进行去中心化数学计算。

Method: Skywing提出由三种抽象组成的编程模型：agents（表示去中心化计算的参与者）、processors（封装算法特定的更新规则）、iterations（管理分布式执行）。平台支持异步操作、发布-订阅通信、受管消息处理和灵活算法组合为复杂去中心化工作流。

Result: 通过共识、优化和数值线性代数等代表性算法进行演示。实验包括Push Sum、Max Consensus、组合式监控与控制工作流，以及在延迟通信和恶意数据损坏下的弹性Push Sum和异步Jacobi算法。结果表明Skywing能够在分离数学逻辑与通信/执行基础设施的同时，支持多样化去中心化算法。

Conclusion: Skywing既可作为去中心化应用的部署框架，也可作为开发弹性数学算法的研究平台，为不可靠网络环境下的分布式数学计算提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Emerging edge, autonomous, and cyber-physical systems increasingly require mathematical computation across heterogeneous devices connected by unreliable communication networks. Traditional high-performance computing and distributed data-processing frameworks provide powerful abstractions for managed environments but are less suited to decentralized settings where centralized coordination, reliable communication, and global synchronization cannot be assumed. This paper presents Skywing, an open-source platform for decentralized mathematical computing in unreliable environments. Its programming model consists of three abstractions: agents represent participants in a decentralized computation, processors encapsulate algorithm-specific update rules, and iterations manage distributed execution. Skywing supports asynchronous operation, publish-subscribe communication, managed message handling, and the composition of independent algorithms into complex decentralized workflows. We demonstrate Skywing using representative algorithms from consensus, optimization, and numerical linear algebra. Experiments on the native Skywing runtime include Push Sum and Max Consensus, a composed monitoring and control workflow, resilient Push Sum under delayed communication, and resilient asynchronous Jacobi under malevolent data corruption. These demonstrations show that Skywing supports diverse decentralized algorithms while separating mathematical logic from communication and execution infrastructure. Skywing serves as both a deployment framework for decentralized applications and a research platform for developing resilient mathematical algorithms.

</details>


### [130] [BASP: Communication-Efficient Batch-Aware Sequence Parallelism for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03151)
*Bigyan Ghimire,Jon C. Calhoun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出BASP（Batch-Aware Sequence Parallelism），一种利用batch结构减少通信开销的序列并行方法，在A100集群上将Llama和Qwen模型的端到端训练时间提升1.17-1.31倍，同时保持相同的模型精度和内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的长序列训练面临巨大的内存和通信瓶颈，现有序列并行方法与batch大小无关，对所有batch规模采用统一的序列划分方式，导致通信效率低下。

Method: BASP根据微批大小将GPU划分为不相交的序列并行组，缩小了all-to-all通信的组规模，从而将通信局部化，减少通信开销并提升训练效率。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100集群上的实验表明，与标准序列并行基线相比，BASP在Llama和Qwen模型上实现了高达1.17-1.31倍的端到端训练时间提升，且模型精度和内存使用保持不变。

Conclusion: 利用batch结构来指导序列并行分组可以有效减少通信开销，提升长序列LLM训练效率，是一种无需牺牲精度和内存的优化方案。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning for large language models (LLMs) is becoming increasingly important, but training over long sequences remains challenging due to massive memory and communication requirements. Sequence parallelism has emerged as an essential technique for addressing bottlenecks in long sequence LLM training. However, we observe that existing sequence parallelism methods are batch-agnostic and apply uniform sequence partitioning across all batch sizes, resulting in inefficient communication. In this paper, we introduce Batch- Aware Sequence Parallelism (BASP), a sequence parallelism approach that leverages batch structure to reduce communication overhead. BASP exploits batch structure by partitioning GPUs into disjoint sequence-parallel groups according to the micro- batch size. This design reduces the all-to-all communication group size, thereby localizing communication and improving training efficiency. Experimental results on an NVIDIA A100 cluster show that BASP improves end-to-end training time by up to 1.17 - 1.31x in Llama and Qwen models compared to standard sequence parallel baselines, while preserving identical model accuracy and memory usage.

</details>


### [131] [Carbon-aware Resource Management for Latency-Sensitive Cloud Computing Environments: A Taxonomy and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03270)
*Tharindu B. Hewage,Shashikant Ilager,Maria Rodriguez Read,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对云环境中延迟敏感型工作负载的碳排放问题，提出了一个详细的碳感知资源管理分类法，并基于该分类法系统分析了现有研究、识别研究空白并指出未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算基础设持续扩展导致碳足迹增加，与净零排放目标相悖。延迟敏感型工作负载的优化往往只考虑性能，缺乏对碳排放的关注。由于延迟优化部署模式刚性，难以降低碳足迹，亟需利用应用特定机会的高效碳减排技术。

Method: 提出了一种针对延迟敏感型云计算环境中碳感知资源管理的详细分类法（taxonomy），利用该分类法对近期相关文献进行系统分析，考察其优化方面的特征。

Result: 通过分类法系统梳理了现有碳感知资源管理研究，分析了各研究的优化维度与方法，揭示了当前文献中存在的空白与不足。

Conclusion: 识别了现有研究的局限与未解决的问题，并为未来在延迟敏感型云计算环境中实现碳感知资源管理的研究方向提供了建议。

Abstract: Proliferation of cloud-based latency-sensitive workloads requires infrastructures tuned to their workload-specific latency constraints. Today, they shape the cloud from a generalized computing platform to diverse workload-specific cloud environments. As the demand for latency-sensitive workloads increases, cloud service providers continue to scale their infrastructure, adversely increasing the carbon footprint and challenging climate-crisis-driven net-zero emission goals. Due to performance-oriented rigid deployment patterns of latency-optimizations, reducing its carbon footprint is challenging. Therefore, efficient techniques that exploit application specific opportunities are needed in that. To this end, we present a detailed taxonomy of recent literature on carbon-aware resource management in latency-sensitive cloud computing environments. Using the taxonomy, we analyze existing works discussing their optimization aspects, identify the gaps, and highlight future research directions.

</details>


### [132] [A Technique for Load Shifting Low-latency Applications in Multi-Region Renewables Harvesting via SMT Core Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03297)
*Tharindu B. Hewage,Shashikant Ilager,Maria A. Rodriguez,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种软硬件协同设计技术，通过硬件层面利用SMT核心与深空闲机制维持静态逻辑核心集，软件层面在区域内高效调度低延迟应用，从而将跨区域负载转移中对低延迟应用的迁移需求降低80%，并将p90端到端延迟变异系数降低43.81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨区域负载转移是降低云基础设施碳排放的常用手段，但由于广域网（WAN）的高延迟波动，低延迟应用往往被排除在负载转移策略之外，导致其无法充分利用可再生能源。现有方法未能有效解决将低延迟应用纳入负载转移时面临的延迟约束问题。

Method: 采用软硬件协同设计：在硬件层面，通过让两个服务器池（其中一个启用SMT）的物理核心深度空闲来匹配可再生能源供应峰值和低谷，获得静态逻辑核心集以减少工作负载迁移概率；在软件层面，在区域内高效追踪静态核心集以服务低延迟应用，同时优先调度尽力而为（best-effort）应用以满足跨WAN的负载转移需求。该方法利用SMT核心因硬件多线程带来的较低性能折衷。

Result: 在OpenStack与CPU空闲状态上实现了所提技术，并使用Azure VM到达轨迹在真实实验测试平台上进行评估。结果显示：低延迟VM的外移量减少了80%，p90端到端延迟变异系数降低了43.81%，因SMT核心导致的最坏延迟折衷为11.97%。

Conclusion: 软硬件协同设计方法能有效将低延迟应用纳入跨区域负载转移框架中，在显著减少低延迟工作负载跨WAN迁移的同时保持可接受的延迟性能，为绿色云计算中兼顾延迟敏感应用与碳减排提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Load shifting across geographic regions to chase intermittent renewable energy availability is commonly used in reducing cloud infrastructure carbon footprint. However, it often omits low-latency applications due to high latency variances of wide area networks (WAN) that interconnect regions. This paper addresses accommodating low-latency applications into load shifting by minimizing their shifting across the WAN. We propose a technique using a hardware-software co-design approach. At the hardware level, we conduct server load matching over renewables supply peaks and valleys by deep idling physical cores in two otherwise identical server pools, with one enabling simultaneous multi-threading (SMT) in CPUs. In return, we achieve a static set of logical cores amidst energy supply dynamics, reducing the probability of workload shifting. At the software level, we efficiently chase the static set of cores for low-latency applications within regions while prioritizing best-effort applications to accommodate shifting requirements across WANs. Our approach exploits the lower performance compromise of SMT cores due to their hardware multi-threading. We implement the proposed technique with OpenStack and CPU idle states and evaluate its performance on a real experimental testbed with Azure VM arrival traces. Results show an 80% reduction in offloading low-latency VMs and a 43.81% reduction in coefficient of variation of p90 end-user latency while having a worst-case latency compromise of 11.97% due to SMT cores.

</details>


### [133] [Lantern: Finding Committable Transactions via Back-Propagation on DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03315)
*Denglong Li,Gerui Wang,Tian Guan,Mingchao Wan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Lantern，一种无需先验知识的确定性并发控制协议。其核心思想是安全地按升序提交依赖图中零出度的事务顶点（采用覆盖宽容策略），并引入反向传播机制迭代识别更多可提交事务，以及冲突免批量选择（CFBS）机制应对读改写密集型场景。在ChainMaker上的评估显示，相比Aria提升最多4.2倍吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并发控制协议要么引入非确定性导致主备节点间存在串行执行回放依赖，要么依赖不切实际的事务读写集先验知识，亟需一种既确定性又无需先验知识的解决方案。

Method: 1) 利用局部依赖图中零出度事务顶点可按升序安全提交的特性（使用覆盖宽容策略）；2) 提出反向传播（Back-Propagation）机制，迭代将依赖状态从汇点向源点传播以发现更多可提交事务；3) 提出冲突免批量选择（CFBS）以应对读改写密集型场景；4) 将协议集成到ChainMaker开源区块链平台中。

Result: 在YCSB和SmallBank基准上的广泛评估表明，Lantern相比Aria实现最高4.2倍吞吐量提升，并使ChainMaker执行层吞吐量至少提升2.2倍。

Conclusion: Lantern通过巧妙的依赖图排序策略和反向传播机制，实现了无需先验读写集知识的确定性并发控制，在高性能事务处理系统中取得了显著的实际性能提升。

Abstract: Existing concurrency control protocols either introduce nondeterminism, resulting in a serial execution-replay dependency between primary and replica nodes, or rely on impractical prior knowledge of transaction read-write sets. In this paper, we present Lantern, a deterministic concurrency control protocol tailored for high-performance transaction processing systems operating without prior knowledge. The key insight of Lantern is that all zero-out-degree transaction vertices in the local dependency graph can be safely committed in ascending order using an overwrite-permissive strategy. We further introduce a novel Back-Propagation mechanism that iteratively propagates dependency states from sink to source vertices to identify additional committable transactions. We also propose Conflict-Free Batch Selection (CFBS) for read-modify-write intensive scenarios. We integrate Lantern into the open-source blockchain platform ChainMaker. Extensive evaluations on YCSB and SmallBank benchmarks demonstrate that Lantern achieves up to a 4.2x throughput speedup over Aria and improves the throughput of ChainMaker's execution layer by at least 2.2x.

</details>


### [134] [Iapetus: Content-Aware Hierarchical Scheduling for Collaborative ViT Inference in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03318)
*Yan Chen,Yunxiang Zhang,Guanjun Jiang,Haiquan Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一个内容感知的分层调度器，用于卫星边缘计算中的分布式Vision Transformer推理，通过联合优化token压缩和层卸载策略，在任务延迟、能量和质量损失之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作推理将分布式资源汇集起来以运行计算密集型的ViT，但模型分区会产生大量中间激活数据传输开销。现有的token压缩和层卸载方法各自面临挑战，需要一种综合考虑内容感知、时间变化约束的统一调度方案。

Method: 采用分层架构：先在星座范围内筛选保留有界候选集，再通过质量预测和联合规划将每个候选细化为完整的token压缩与层卸载轨迹。统一目标函数平衡延迟、能耗和质量损失，并考虑累积工作负载和电池压力。

Result: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin硬件在环测试台上实现，并以5任务/秒的速率完成91.6%的发布任务，比最强的基线MARATD3高出26.1个百分点，同时将平均延迟和电池消耗分别降低53.0%和70.8%，并满足质量目标。

Conclusion: 该调度器通过内容感知的联合规划，在动态卫星边缘环境中显著提升了任务完成率、降低了延迟和电池消耗，同时满足质量约束，验证了分层调度方案在协作ViT推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Collaborative inference pools distributed resources to run compute-intensive Vision Transformers (ViTs) in satellite edge computing. Model partitioning enables such collaboration by assigning consecutive layer groups to different nodes, but the large volume of intermediate activation data incurs substantial transfer overhead that can erase its benefit. Token compression reduces downstream computation and activation transfer, but its quality impact depends on input content, model depth, and earlier pruning decisions, while layer offloading must adapt to time-varying contact and battery conditions. We present \sys, a content-aware hierarchical scheduler that screens constellation-wide options to retain a bounded candidate set, then refines each candidate into a complete token compression and layer offloading trajectory using quality prediction and joint planning. A unified objective balances per-task latency, energy, and quality loss against accumulated workload and battery pressures. We implement \sys on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin hardware-in-the-loop testbed and use its validated execution model for constellation-scale trace replay across multiple ViT workloads and constellation settings. At \(5\)~tasks/s, \sys accomplishes 91.6\% of released tasks, 26.1 percentage points above MARATD3, the strongest baseline, while reducing mean latency and battery draw by 53.0\% and 70.8\%, respectively, and meeting quality targets.

</details>


### [135] [Latency-Aware Orchestration for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows on Heterogeneous GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03335)
*Jinghao Wang,Yifeng Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Yao Lu,Yihui Zhang,Xiaoyang Sun,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预测引导的运行时系统，利用工作流预测来构建和优化物理执行图，在异构GPU池上并发多智能体工作流场景下，相比最先进的工作流调度器，端到端执行时间最多降低36.8%，p95完成延迟最多降低25.9%，每个会话节省最多24.63 GPU秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发多智能体工作流在异构GPU池上运行时面临时间变化的负载、模型驻留和资源可用性等挑战。逻辑工作流定义了所需的计算，但物理调度单元（模型生命周期动作、资源排序和放置）必须根据观测到的池状态进行选择，现有方法未能有效联合优化这些方面。

Method: 提出一个预测引导的运行时系统，包含三个核心组件：(1) Predictor估计设备特定的激活延迟、峰值内存和模型加载成本，并通过工作流依赖关系传播预测以预测激活就绪状态和未来模型需求；(2) Constructor构建保持语义的融合和模型生命周期替代方案；(3) Scheduler基于实时池状态联合优化这些方案的选择、放置和执行顺序。

Result: 在覆盖三个工作流场景的异构GPU池工作负载中，系统相比最先进的工作流调度器，在突发到达下将端到端执行时间（makespan）最多降低36.8%，整体p95完成延迟最多降低25.9%，每个完成的会话最多节省24.63 GPU秒。

Conclusion: 预测引导的物理执行图构建与优化方法能够有效应对异构GPU池上的并发工作流调度挑战，通过预测、构造和协同调度三个阶段的紧密配合，在降低完成延迟和节约GPU资源方面均取得了显著效果。

Abstract: Concurrent multi-agent workflows expose future dependencies and serving-state requirements while running on heterogeneous GPU pools with time-varying load, model residency, and resource availability. The logical workflow defines the required computation, whereas its physical scheduling units, model-lifecycle actions, resource ordering, and placement must be selected according to the observed pool state. We present a prediction-guided runtime that uses workflow forecasts to construct and optimize a physical execution graph. Predictor estimates device-specific activation latency, peak memory, and model-loading cost, then propagates these predictions through workflow dependencies to forecast activation readiness and future model demand. Constructor builds semantics-preserving fusion and model-lifecycle alternatives, while Scheduler jointly optimizes their selection, placement, and execution order based on the live pool state. Across a workload spanning three workflow scenarios on a heterogeneous GPU pool, our system reduces end-to-end makespan and overall p95 completion latency under burst arrivals by up to 36.8% and 25.9%, respectively, over state-of-the-art workflow schedulers. It also saves up to 24.63 GPU-s per completed session.

</details>


### [136] [FlowTT: Exploiting Computation Flow Reuse in Irregular Tensor-Train Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03459)
*Jongmin Seok,Chae Eun Rhee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlowTT是一个面向GPU的流感知执行框架，通过将Tensor-Train（TT）嵌入查找重构为一组前缀共享的不规则计算流，结合流对齐的前缀索引分组、融合的TT-embedding执行路径以及持久线程调度等技术，在Meta推荐基准上相比现有方法将推理延迟降低最高42.2%、训练延迟降低最高49.2%，同时实现最低的推理峰值内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TT分解能有效压缩推荐模型中的大型嵌入表，但TT嵌入查找效率低下：跨输入索引的部分共享计算流未得到充分复用，且顺序TT核收缩之间中间结果被反复物化到片外，造成大量冗余计算和内存流量。

Method: FlowTT提出三类协同设计：1）流对齐的前缀索引分组，将共享计算路径的输入归类；2）融合TT-embedding执行路径，在片上保留中间结果以避免反复物化到片外；3）持久线程调度结合基于块的工作窃取和L2检查点，在偏斜工作负载下保持复用性。

Result: 在Meta合成推荐基准（Meta-240、Meta-480和Meta-788）上，FlowTT在所有方法中实现最低延迟。批大小32,768时，相比EcoRec，推理延迟降低最多42.2%，训练延迟降低最多49.2%，且推理峰值内存使用量最低。

Conclusion: 暴露前缀共享计算是高效TT嵌入执行的关键。通过协同设计任务形成、数据缓冲和调度策略与TT gather的结构特征，FlowTT能有效减少冗余TT核操作、全局内存流量和负载不平衡。

Abstract: Tensor-Train (TT) decomposition effectively compresses large embedding tables in recommendation models, but TT-based embedding lookup remains inefficient because partially shared computation flows across input indices are not fully reused and intermediate results are repeatedly materialized off-chip between sequential TT-core contractions. We present FlowTT, a flow-aware GPU execution framework that reformulates TT gather as a set of prefix-shared irregular computation flows. FlowTT combines flow-aligned prefix-based index grouping, a fused TT-embedding execution path with on-chip intermediate retention, and persistent-thread scheduling with chunk-based work stealing and L2 checkpointing to preserve reuse under skewed workloads. By co-designing task formation, data buffering, and scheduling with the structure of TT gather, FlowTT reduces redundant TT-core operations, global-memory traffic, and load imbalance. On Meta's synthetic recommendation benchmarks (Meta-240, Meta-480, and Meta-788), FlowTT consistently achieves the lowest latency compared to existing methods. At batch size 32,768, it reduces latency by up to 42.2% in inference and 49.2% in training relative to EcoRec, while also achieving the lowest inference peak memory usage. These results show that exposing prefix-shared computation is key to efficient TT-based embedding execution.

</details>


### [137] [JuPyLive: Seamless Migration of Jupyter Notebook Resources from Laptop to HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03562)
*Sima Attar-Khorasani,Matthias Lieber,Siavash Ghiasvand*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出JuPyLive，一种使Jupyter笔记本能够在用户本地工作站与HPC远程资源之间无缝迁移的机制。它基于ElasticNotebook管理内存状态迁移，自动分配HPC集群资源并编排通信通道，支持双向迁移。用户无需修改代码或学习新语法，只需点击即可在熟悉的Jupyter环境中完成迁移，并可通过HPC状态监视器了解可用资源后做出选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员在使用Jupyter笔记本进行资源密集型工作时，常常面临本地资源不足而HPC集群使用门槛高的问题。现有的从本地到HPC的迁移过程复杂且繁琐，需要用户掌握额外知识并手动配置，阻碍了跨学科研究人员对HPC集群的充分利用。

Method: JuPyLive采用以下核心技术：(1) 利用ElasticNotebook实现内存状态的迁移管理；(2) 自动化HPC集群上的资源分配；(3) 编排源与目标之间的远程通信通道以支持双向迁移；(4) 提供HPC状态监视器，实时展示可用远程资源供用户决策；(5) 通过Jupyter界面内的可视化元素实现直观的迁移启动与监控，全程无需代码变更或配置。

Result: JuPyLive实现了完全自动化的迁移机制，用户无需修改代码、无需额外配置、也无需学习新语法。通过桥接本地工作空间与远程HPC资源，该机制以最小程度的用户干预即可实现本地资源密集型工作流的无缝扩展。

Conclusion: JuPyLive通过弥合本地工作空间与远程HPC资源之间的差距，为跨学科研究人员提供了无缝的扩展体验，以极低的使用门槛推动了HPC集群的普及化应用（democratization）。

Abstract: This work introduces JuPyLive, a migration mechanism that enables seamless transition of Jupyter notebooks between local resources of user's workstation and remote resources of high-performance computing~(HPC) environments, while preserving the user experience. JuPyLive eliminates the underlying complexities of migration process, enabling users to freely choose among available local and remote resources, directly within the familiar Jupyter notebook environment via a single click. JuPyLive leverages ElasticNotebook to manage in-memory state migration, it automates resource allocation on HPC cluster and orchestrates required remote communication channels between the source and destination to enable a bidirectional migration. Furthermore, HPC status monitor of JuPyLive provides a live overview of available remote resources, allowing users to make informed decisions on choosing the relevant resources before initiating a migration process. The proposed fully automatic mechanism requires no code changes or configurations by the end user, nor does it demand users to learn a new syntax, instead the migration process can be intuitively initiated and monitored using visual elements from within the Jupyter notebook. By bridging the gap between local workspace and remote resources, JuPyLive offers a seamless experience for scaling local resource-intensive workflows with minimal user intervention, thus further democratizing the usage of HPC clusters among the interdisciplinary researchers.

</details>


### [138] [Every Kernel Is a Join: Automatic Multi-GPU Parallelism for AI Computations in Einsummable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03905)
*Zhimin Ding,Chen-Kuan Liao,Chima Adiole,Brianna Barrow,Fangzhou Du,Yu Hsiao,Ge Huang,Yicheng Jin,Ismail Syed,Chris Jermaine*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Einsummable是一个原型系统，能接受类似PyTorch的AI计算描述并自动在多GPU服务器上分发，无需程序员编写设备分配、分片标注或通信操作，通过将每个操作建模为关系连接加聚合（join-agg specs）来实现自动并行化，在LLaMA transformer块上优于手工调优的PyTorch和vLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
